P0141: 2.1Epic Narratives
P0142: 2.1.1EP-01: Foundation (Phases 00–03) — Done
P0143: Close the design report's open decisions, retire the technical risks (.NET 10 matrix, PDFium wrapper, WebView bridge, FTS5, AI gateway), and stand up the 9-project Clean/Onion solution with warnings-as-errors, architecture tests, the golden-corpus harness, design tokens, the icon system, and the en/fr i18n scaffold. Evidence: commits of 2026-05-30/31 ("ratify ADR-0001..0010", "scaffold 9-project solution", "Phase 03 design tokens, IconCatalog"); ADR-0001 through ADR-0009 ratified.
P0144: 2.1.2EP-02: Library Setup and Scanning (Phases 04–05) — Done
P0145: The catalogue of record (SQLite via EF Core, sidecar layout) plus the ingestion pipeline: root selection, recursive discovery, hash-based identity, unavailable-file preservation, background thumbnails and spines, incremental rescan, and the scan health report. Key code: IngestionOrchestrator, BookIdentityService, BookIngestionWorker.
P0146: 2.1.3EP-03: Catalogue Browsing (Phase 06) — Done except Work/Edition
P0147: Grid, list, and directory views, combinable filters, virtual and smart shelves, the full-metadata inspector, and previewed undoable bulk edit. The Work/Edition duplicate-grouping model (FR-CAT-007, V2) has no implementing code and stays Planned.
P0148: 2.1.4EP-04: Metadata Enrichment and Collection Health (Phase 07) — Done
P0149: ISBN detection and ranking, Google Books and Open Library lookups with provenance and confidence, the reviewable merge policy, reversible PDF write-back (backup, diff, verify, restore), batch enrichment with rate limits, the metadata quality score, and the library health dashboard (LibraryHealthService).
P0150: 2.1.5EP-05: Reader, Annotations, and Power Tools (Phases 08, 09, 15) — Done except split view
P0151: PDFium rendering with page cache and resume, navigation, zoom and display modes, full-screen, in-document search, bookmarks, durable highlights and notes, password-protected PDFs via OS credential flow, background OCR (Tesseract), citation capture and research export, reading memory, and annotation layers. Split view (FR-READ-012, V2) was in the Phase 15 plan but was not built; it is carried as planned scope.
P0152: 2.1.6EP-06: Search and Indexing (Phases 10–11) — Done
P0153: Metadata search inside the NFR-OGMA-003 budget, the FTS5 external-content index, extraction pipeline, match-location explanations, local embeddings, semantic search, hybrid ranking, and the index manager (FtsIndexService, SemanticSearchService, HybridRankingService, IndexManagerService).
P0154: 2.1.7EP-07: AI Gateway, Privacy, and Advisor (Phases 12–13) — Done except answer mode
P0155: The provider-neutral gateway (Anthropic, OpenAI-compatible, Ollama) behind the single egress chokepoint, four privacy tiers with payload preview, consent, cost display, local history with erasure, the Privacy Center, explainable recommendations, and reading plans. The V2 evidence-cited answer mode (FR-AI-008) is a deliberate NotImplementedException stub in AdvisorService and stays Planned.
P0156: 2.1.8EP-08: 3D Bookshelf (Phase 14) — Done
P0157: Three.js r181 hosted in WebView2/WKWebView with a typed C#-to-JavaScript bridge, the ogma:// asset scheme, generated spine textures, and grid/list parity as the accessible fallback.
P0158: 2.1.9EP-09: LAN Library Host Mode (Phase 16) — Done
P0159: Opt-in Kestrel host serving catalogue metadata and range-request PDF streams over TLS, mDNS discovery, certificate provisioning with a trust-on-first-use client store, enrollment codes and QR join links, session management, publish-scope enforcement, and the exportable host audit log.
P0160: 2.1.10EP-10: Classroom Client Mode (Phase 17) — Done
P0161: Host discovery and connection, per-student profiles with private reading state, roles, streamed reading without full-file copies, the bounded offline cache, reading-state sync with conflict rules, host-index search with match explanations, revocation semantics, read-only guarantees, entitlements, and the aggregate-only teacher dashboard.
P0162: 2.1.11EP-11: School Administration and Managed AI (Phase 18) — Done
P0163: Publish/unpublish, shared shelves, profile enrollment and revocation, school AI keys in the host OS credential store, the host AI proxy, metadata-only class default with student payload preview, quotas and rate limits, the usage dashboard, classroom answer citations scoped to the host catalogue, student history deletion, and institution-wide purge.
P0164: 2.1.12EP-12: Premium UX (Phases 03 and 21) — Mixed
P0165: Zero-configuration onboarding, loading/empty/error/in-progress states, themes and reader typography, and en/fr resources are implemented; the ≤ 5 min onboarding and 60 s locate-and-resume journeys await formal measurement on W-REF-01 at Phase 20, and the WCAG 2.2 AA keyboard/screen-reader audit is Phase 21 scope. The command palette (FR-UX-003) and in-app help (FR-UX-006) have no implementing code and are Planned.
P0166: 2.1.13EP-13 through EP-17: Remaining Work (Phases 19–23) — Planned
P0167: Security hardening (threat-model execution, untrusted-PDF isolation hardening, at-rest encryption, SAST), formal NFR acceptance on the reference hardware, the WCAG 2.2 AA audit with Spanish/Italian/German localization, packaging and store submission (Velopack, MSIX, notarized DMG per ADR-0009), and beta/launch operations with the extension SDK, local read API, and importers. These epics are the subject of the forward sprint plan.
P0168: 3User Stories — Ogma Library
P0169: Each story states a role-goal-benefit outcome, the baselined requirements it implements, deterministic acceptance criteria, a Fibonacci story-point estimate, an implementation status, and an owner by role. Personas come from _context/stakeholders.md. Status values agree with _context/build-status.md:
P0170: Code/test evidence — the cited behavior exists in the inspected source and is exercised by automated tests; release acceptance may remain open.
P0171: In verification — implemented; formal acceptance evidence (reference-hardware measurement or WCAG audit) is Phase 20–21 scope.
P0172: Planned — not implemented; scheduled in the forward sprint plan (Phases 19–23 or V2).
P0173: Points for Done stories are retrospective estimates recorded for forecasting comparability, not measured effort. Coverage: 59 stories cover all 101 baselined functional requirements; the rollup is in 09-governance-compliance/01-traceability-matrix/traceability-matrix.md.
P0174: 3.1EP-01 — Foundation
P0175: 3.1.1US-001: Retire the technical risks before committing delivery
P0176: As the engineering lead, I want the .NET 10 dependency matrix, PDFium wrapper benchmark, WebView-to-Three.js bridge, FTS5 prototype, AI-gateway skeleton, and LAN transport proven in spikes, so that no launch-critical dependency rests on an assumption. Implements the Phase 00–01 decision closure feeding ADR-0001 through ADR-0009.
P0177: Acceptance: each spike produces a committed proof under tests/ or docs/plans/grand-plan/phase-01/ and an accepted ADR or ADR amendment.
P0178: Acceptance: git log shows the ratification commit ("docs: ratify ADR-0001..0010", 2026-05-30) and spike-proof commit ("test(spikes)", 2026-05-30).
P0179: Points: 8. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0180: 3.1.2US-002: Stand up the solution skeleton and quality gates
P0181: As the engineering lead, I want a 9-project Clean/Onion solution with warnings-as-errors, XML-doc enforcement, a single DI composition root, NetArchTest architecture tests, CI, the golden-corpus harness, design tokens, the icon catalog, and the en/fr string scaffold, so that every later phase builds on an enforced architecture. Supports NFR-PROD-011 (signed builds, idempotent migrations) and the single HTTP-egress chokepoint behind CTRL-016.
P0182: Acceptance: dotnet build passes with warnings-as-errors; OgmaLibrary.Tests.Architecture enforces inward dependencies and the egress chokepoint.
P0183: Acceptance: pseudolocale tests pass against the en/fr resource files under src/OgmaLibrary.App/Assets/Strings/.
P0184: Points: 8. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0185: 3.2EP-02 — Library Setup and Scanning (FR-LIB)
P0186: 3.2.1US-003: Choose a root and scan it incrementally
P0187: As a serious reader, I want to pick a library folder and have Ogma discover every PDF under it — quickly on re-scan — so that my collection becomes a catalogue without manual entry. Implements FR-LIB-001, FR-LIB-002, FR-LIB-006.
P0188: Acceptance: a root with nested folders yields one catalogue row per discovered PDF; excluded folders yield zero rows.
P0189: Acceptance: an incremental rescan of an unchanged 2,000-file tree re-reads no file content (modification-time/size/hash cache hit rate 100% in the ingestion log).
P0190: Points: 5. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0191: 3.2.2US-004: Never lose my books' identity or my notes
P0192: As a collector, I want renamed or moved files re-matched and missing files preserved, so that shelves, notes, and progress survive file-system churn. Implements FR-LIB-003, FR-LIB-004.
P0193: Acceptance: renaming a file with unchanged content hash re-matches to the same book row (same book ID) after rescan.
P0194: Acceptance: deleting a file marks the book unavailable while its shelves, annotations, and progress rows remain queryable.
P0195: Points: 8. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0196: 3.2.3US-005: Generate covers and spines without freezing the app
P0197: As a serious reader, I want thumbnails and spine textures produced in background jobs, so that browsing stays responsive during a large import. Implements FR-LIB-005; supports NFR-PROD-004 (non-blocking background jobs) and NFR-OGMA-009 (job recovery).
P0198: Acceptance: the catalogue view accepts input while BookIngestionWorker jobs run; a killed job resumes after restart without duplicate work.
P0199: Acceptance: a failed thumbnail job appears in the job list with a working retry action.
P0200: Points: 5. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0201: 3.2.4US-006: Audit scan health
P0202: As a power librarian, I want a report of failed, password-protected, and unsupported files plus missing thumbnails and metadata gaps, so that I can maintain a large collection deliberately. Implements FR-LIB-007.
P0203: Acceptance: the report lists each category with a count and a per-item link that opens the affected book's detail.
P0204: Points: 3. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0205: 3.3EP-03 — Catalogue Browsing (FR-CAT)
P0206: 3.3.1US-007: Browse, sort, filter, and inspect in equivalent views
P0207: As a serious reader, I want grid, list, and directory views with combinable filters and a full-metadata inspector, so that I can find any book without remembering file names. Implements FR-CAT-001 (2D views), FR-CAT-002, FR-CAT-004; supports NFR-OGMA-002 (catalogue load ≤ 2 s for 2,000 books).
P0208: Acceptance: the same book opens the same detail and reader from every view (view-equivalence test).
P0209: Acceptance: applying title + tag + status filters returns the set intersection; clearing filters restores the full catalogue.
P0210: Points: 8. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0211: 3.3.2US-008: Organise with virtual and smart shelves
P0212: As a serious reader, I want manual shelves plus saved-filter smart shelves, so that one book can live in many collections that maintain themselves. Implements FR-CAT-003, FR-CAT-006 (SmartShelfCondition and evaluator).
P0213: Acceptance: a book added to 2 shelves appears in both; removing it from one leaves the other membership intact.
P0214: Acceptance: changing a book's status updates smart-shelf membership on next evaluation without user action.
P0215: Points: 5. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0216: 3.3.3US-009: Bulk edit with preview and undo
P0217: As a power librarian, I want previewed, undoable bulk edits of tags, shelves, status, and confidence, so that I can curate hundreds of books safely. Implements FR-CAT-005.
P0218: Acceptance: a bulk edit of 50 books shows the exact field diff before commit; undo restores every prior value.
P0219: Points: 5. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0220: 3.3.4US-010: Group duplicates under one Work
P0221: As a collector, I want duplicate files and editions grouped under one intellectual Work with merge and split, so that my catalogue counts works, not file copies. Implements FR-CAT-007 (V2).
P0222: Acceptance: two files of the same title merge into one Work carrying both editions; split restores two independent rows with their annotations intact.
P0223: Points: 8. Status: Planned. Owner: engineering lead.
P0224: 3.4EP-04 — Metadata Enrichment (FR-META)
P0225: 3.4.1US-011: Detect identifiers and fetch candidates
P0226: As a collector, I want ISBNs detected from filename, XMP/DocInfo, and first pages, then looked up in Google Books and Open Library, so that my metadata repairs itself from authoritative sources. Implements FR-META-001, FR-META-002.
P0227: Acceptance: an ISBN-10 in a filename normalises to ISBN-13, validates its check digit, and ranks above a low-confidence body-text hit.
P0228: Acceptance: every lookup result stores source, timestamp, and confidence in MetadataLookups.
P0229: Points: 5. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0230: 3.4.2US-012: Merge reviewably with provenance
P0231: As a collector, I want candidate metadata merged through a confidence policy I can review, with field-level provenance, so that no provider silently overwrites my corrections. Implements FR-META-003, FR-META-004.
P0232: Acceptance: a conflicting title from two providers surfaces both values with confidence; a manual edit wins over any later provider value.
P0233: Points: 8. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0234: 3.4.3US-013: Write back to PDF only reversibly
P0235: As a collector, I want metadata write-back to back up the original, show a field diff, verify the file reopens, and restore on failure, so that enrichment can never corrupt a book. Implements FR-META-005; supports NFR-PROD-010 and CTRL-011.
P0236: Acceptance: fault injection during write-back leaves the original byte-identical and surfaces the restore path (PdfWriteBackService fault tests).
P0237: Points: 8. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0238: 3.4.4US-014: Enrich in batches and see collection health
P0239: As a power librarian, I want rate-limited batch enrichment, a metadata quality score, and a health dashboard with remediation actions, so that a 2,000-book collection is auditable in one surface. Implements FR-META-006, FR-META-007, FR-META-008 (LibraryHealthService).
P0240: Acceptance: a paused batch job is visible and resumable; provider rate limits bound request frequency in the gateway log.
P0241: Acceptance: the dashboard lists duplicates, missing metadata, unavailable files, broken covers, and index gaps, each with a working remediation action.
P0242: Points: 8. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0243: 3.5EP-05 — Reader, Annotations, and Power Tools (FR-READ)
P0244: 3.5.1US-015: Open, resume, and control the page
P0245: As a serious reader, I want books to reopen at my last position with full navigation, zoom, display modes, and full-screen, so that reading is comfortable across sessions. Implements FR-READ-001, FR-READ-002, FR-READ-003, FR-READ-004, FR-READ-005; supports NFR-OGMA-005 (page turn ≤ 100 ms cached).
P0246: Acceptance: closing a 400-page book at page 217 and reopening lands on page 217 with the persisted zoom and display mode.
P0247: Acceptance: first/previous/next/last/jump/history navigation works by keyboard and UI; Escape exits full-screen.
P0248: Points: 8. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0249: 3.5.2US-016: Search inside the document
P0250: As a researcher, I want in-document text search with highlighted matches and a page list, so that I can jump to the passage I remember. Implements FR-READ-006.
P0251: Acceptance: a query on a text-layer PDF highlights every match and lists matching pages; selecting a page navigates to it.
P0252: Points: 5. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0253: 3.5.3US-017: Keep durable annotations, memory, and layers
P0254: As a researcher, I want bookmarks, highlights, notes, per-book reading memory, and named annotation layers that persist exactly, so that my reading produces durable artefacts. Implements FR-READ-007, FR-READ-008, FR-READ-014, FR-READ-015; supports NFR-OGMA-008 (annotation durability).
P0255: Acceptance: annotations reload at identical positions after close/reopen (round-trip test in OgmaLibrary.Reader).
P0256: Acceptance: hiding the "quotes" layer hides only its annotations; layer export contains only that layer's items.
P0257: Points: 8. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0258: 3.5.4US-018: Handle protected and scanned documents
P0259: As a researcher, I want password-protected PDFs opened through the OS credential flow and scanned PDFs OCR-indexed in the background, so that difficult sources stay usable. Implements FR-READ-009, FR-READ-010 (Tesseract 5.2.0 workers).
P0260: Acceptance: a password PDF prompts once with consent, then opens; the credential lives in the OS store, never in the catalogue (CTRL-001).
P0261: Acceptance: an image-only PDF becomes searchable after the OCR job and its hits are labelled OCR-derived.
P0262: Points: 8. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0263: 3.5.5US-019: Capture citations and export research
P0264: As a researcher, I want citation cards (title, author, page, selected text) and export to BibTeX, RIS, CSL JSON, and Markdown digest, so that reading feeds my writing without retyping. Implements FR-READ-011, FR-READ-013 (CitationService, sidecar export).
P0265: Acceptance: a citation card carries all four fields; a BibTeX export of 3 selected books parses in a reference manager without error.
P0266: Points: 5. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0267: 3.5.6US-020: Read two locations side by side
P0268: As a researcher, I want two PDFs, or two locations in one PDF, side by side, so that I can compare sources directly. Implements FR-READ-012 (V2; carried from the Phase 15 plan, not built).
P0269: Acceptance: both panes navigate and zoom independently; annotations land in the correct document.
P0270: Points: 5. Status: Planned. Owner: engineering lead.
P0271: 3.6EP-06 — Search and Indexing (FR-SEARCH)
P0272: 3.6.1US-021: Search metadata as I type and full text when I ask
P0273: As a researcher, I want metadata search that answers while typing and an FTS5 index over text, notes, tags, descriptions, and tables of contents, so that I move from intent to source in seconds. Implements FR-SEARCH-001, FR-SEARCH-002; supports NFR-OGMA-003 (≤ 150 ms) and NFR-OGMA-004 (≤ 500 ms warm).
P0274: Acceptance: keypress-to-render on a 2,000-book catalogue meets the NFR-OGMA-003 budget in the query-replay benchmark (trend evidence until Phase 20).
P0275: Acceptance: a phrase occurring only in extracted body text returns its book with the containing page.
P0276: Points: 8. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0277: 3.6.2US-022: Understand and improve every match
P0278: As a researcher, I want match-location explanations, natural-language semantic search, and hybrid ranking, so that results are trustworthy and intent-tolerant. Implements FR-SEARCH-003, FR-SEARCH-004, FR-SEARCH-005 (SemanticSearchService, HybridRankingService).
P0279: Acceptance: each result names its match location (title, author, note, text-page, or semantic hit).
P0280: Acceptance: a natural-language query with no exact term overlap returns semantically related books ranked by the hybrid blend.
P0281: Points: 8. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: AI engineer.
P0282: 3.6.3US-023: Manage the index
P0283: As a power librarian, I want an index manager showing progress, indexed books, pending OCR, failures, and index size, with rebuild-from-source, so that search health is observable and repairable. Implements FR-SEARCH-006 (IndexManagerService).
P0284: Acceptance: deleting the index files and rebuilding from source restores identical result sets for a fixed query battery.
P0285: Points: 5. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0286: 3.7EP-07 — AI Gateway, Privacy, and Advisor (FR-AI)

Implements FR-AI-001, FR-AI-002 (AiGateway, AiDisabledProvider default).

P0290: Acceptance: the same recommendation request routes through a cloud provider and Ollama with no contract change (gateway integration test).

P0300: Points: 5. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: AI engineer.
P0301: 3.7.4US-027: Get explained recommendations and reading plans

P0321: 3.9EP-09 — LAN Library Host Mode (FR-LAN)

P0323: As a school administrator, I want host mode off by default and — when I enable it — TLS serving, mDNS advertisement, and certificate provisioning with client-side trust-on-first-use fingerprints, so that sharing is deliberate and authenticated. P0327: 3.9.2US-032: Enroll clients and stream books safely
P0328: As a school administrator, I want per-profile credentials with QR join codes, session listing/limits/revocation, and range-request streaming validated against the published root, so that only enrolled clients read only published files. P0329: Acceptance: an unenrolled client receives 401 on every content route; a revoked session loses access at next request.

P0332: 3.9.3US-033: Publish deliberately, degrade safely, audit everything
P0333: As a school administrator, I want only published folders/shelves enumerable, host outages to be non-destructive for clients, and an exportable activity log, so that operation is controlled and reviewable. Implements FR-LAN-008, FR-LAN-009, FR-LAN-010.
P0334: Acceptance: unpublished content is absent from every catalogue and search response, not merely hidden in UI.
P0335: Acceptance: after a host restart, reconnecting clients re-sync reading state with no data loss; the audit export contains connections, streams, and denials.
P0336: Points: 8. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0337: 3.10EP-10 — Classroom Client Mode (FR-CLIENT)
P0338: 3.10.1US-034: Join a classroom and read streamed books
P0339: As a student, I want to discover or enter a host, browse the shared catalogue, and read by streaming without full-file copies, so that any LAN computer becomes my library seat. Implements FR-CLIENT-001, FR-CLIENT-004 (ClassroomClient). Supports NFR-CLIENT-001 and NFR-CLIENT-002 (client catalogue load and cached page rendering).
P0340: Acceptance: a join link or discovered host yields the shared catalogue; opening a book streams pages with no complete file materialised outside the managed cache.
P0341: Points: 8. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0342: 3.10.2US-035: Keep my reading state private to my profile
P0343: As a student, I want my own profile with private progress, bookmarks, and notes, role-scoped capabilities, and no visibility of my state or AI history to other students, so that shared hardware does not mean shared privacy. Implements FR-CLIENT-002, FR-CLIENT-003, FR-CLIENT-008 (student private database). Supports NFR-CLIENT-003 (session-token custody in the OS credential store).
P0344: Acceptance: two profiles on one machine see disjoint reading state; a student role cannot invoke teacher or admin capabilities.
P0345: Points: 8. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.
P0346: 3.10.3US-036: Keep reading when the host disappears
P0347: As a student, I want a size-limited offline cache with purge control and a visible connection state that degrades to my local library, so that a host outage never strands me mid-chapter. Implements FR-CLIENT-005, FR-CLIENT-011 (disk offline cache).
P0348: Acceptance: with the host unreachable, cached books open and the client shows offline state; the cache respects its configured size limit and purge action.
P0349: Points: 5. Status: Code/test evidence. Owner: engineering lead.

P0504: 4Definition of Ready
P0505: A story enters a sprint only when every condition below holds. The Definition of Ready enforces the hybrid rule that Agile work descends from the baselined Software Requirements Specification. This file is the single source for readiness criteria; definitions/dor-dod.md is the hybrid coupling pointer only and defines nothing of its own.
P0506: 4.1Story Completeness Criteria
P0507: The story traces to at least one baselined requirement (FR-, NFR-, or CTRL-*) and serves a business goal in the range BG-001 through BG-005; the trace is recorded in _registry/baseline-trace.yaml.
P0508: The story states a role-goal-benefit outcome using a named persona from _context/stakeholders.md (serious reader, researcher, collector, power librarian, privacy-sensitive user, student, teacher, school administrator) or a named team role — never "as a user".
P0509: The story carries a unique identifier (US-###) and names its epic.
P0510: 4.2Acceptance Criteria Requirements
P0511: Every acceptance criterion is a deterministic pass/fail check with concrete data values (for example "page 217 of a 400-page book", "2,000-book catalogue"), not "works correctly".
P0512: Both the success path and at least one failure, empty, or boundary path are specified for any story touching persistence, networking, or off-device transmission.
P0513: Performance-touching stories name the specific NFR budget and the measurement definition from _context/reference-hardware.md (for example NFR-OGMA-005, page turn ≤ 100 ms cached, P95 on W-REF-01).
P0514: 4.3Sizing and Estimation Criteria
P0515: The story is estimated in Fibonacci points (3, 5, 8, 13) by the team; anything above 13 is split before commitment.
P0516: The story fits within a single 2-week sprint at the stated team capacity.
P0517: 4.4Dependency Resolution Criteria
P0518: External dependencies (provider stubs, OS credential-store behaviour, reference-hardware availability, owner icon procurement) are identified with a confirmed resolution date or a mitigation.
P0519: Any [CONTEXT-GAP] blocking the story's scope is resolved or explicitly deferred with an owner decision recorded.
P0520: 4.5Design Clarity Criteria
P0521: User-facing stories name the surfaces and states they touch (loading, empty, error, in-progress per FR-UX-002) and their icon-manifest entries.
P0522: Privacy and security implications are noted: the applicable privacy tier, the CTRL-* controls in scope, and — for classroom features — whether minors' data is processed (which pulls the DPIA at 09-governance-compliance/03-dpia/ into review).
P0523: 4.6Refinement Process
P0524: Backlog refinement runs once per sprint, led by the engineering lead with the product owner present.
P0525: A story failing any criterion above returns to refinement with the failing criterion named; readiness is never waived verbally.
P0526: Escalation: a story blocked on an owner decision for more than one sprint is raised at the phase gate with a waiver request (max 90 days) or descoped.
P0527: 5Definition of Done
P0528: A story is done only when every condition below holds. The Definition of Done binds Agile delivery to the baselined quality, accessibility, and compliance bar, and incorporates the grand plan's global Definition of Done (docs/plans/grand-plan/README.md §6 in the codebase), which governed the Phases 00–18 build and continues to govern Phases 19–23. This file is the single source for done criteria; definitions/dor-dod.md is the hybrid coupling pointer only.
P0529: 5.1Story-Level Criteria
P0530: 5.1.1Code Quality
P0531: dotnet format --verify-no-changes, dotnet build with warnings-as-errors, and dotnet test all pass; XML documentation is present on public members (GenerateDocumentationFile=true).
P0532: Architecture tests pass: inward-only dependencies and the single HTTP-egress chokepoint (OgmaLibrary.Tests.Architecture), so no code path bypasses the AI gateway (CTRL-016).
P0533: No hard-coded user-facing string: all new strings are externalized and present in both English and French; the pseudolocale check passes. (Until the OGMA0001 Roslyn analyzer exists, this is enforced by convention plus pseudolocale tests — see the retrospective action A-2.)
P0534: 5.1.2Testing
P0535: Every in-scope requirement ID (FR/NFR/CTRL) touched by the story has a passing deterministic test in the appropriate layer (unit/integration, headless UI, architecture), or a recorded, tagged coverage gap.
P0536: The golden-corpus suite passes; no open R1 (data-loss) or R2 (privacy-breach) defect exists.
P0537: Relevant SRS NFR budgets (for example NFR-OGMA-002 catalogue load) are instrumented and within budget, or recorded as trend data pending Phase 20 reference-hardware acceptance.
P0538: 5.1.3Accessibility and UX
P0539: Every new interactive control carries a colorful icon and an accessible text/aria label; keyboard and screen-reader walkthrough of the touched flow passes (WCAG 2.2 Level AA target, NFR-PROD-007, NFR-PROD-008).
P0540: The phase's icon manifest is complete: procured icons are wired in, or placeholders are flagged with a tracking item (premium icon procurement from the owner is an open release blocker).
P0541: 5.1.4Compliance and Privacy
P0542: The applicable CTRL-001 through CTRL-024 controls hold, verified by test or inspection; stories touching credentials, untrusted documents, or off-device transmission pass a security review.
P0543: A story touching personal data or off-device transmission has a GDPR-aligned lawful basis, consent capture where consent is the basis, and the payload-preview control (CTRL-017, CTRL-019); classroom stories touching students' data conform to the DPIA at 09-governance-compliance/03-dpia/.
P0544: 5.1.5Documentation and Governance
P0545: New or amended ADRs are recorded; affected reference docs, the repo CLAUDE.md, and CHANGELOG.md are updated in the same change (the Phases 00–18 build let these drift — retrospective action A-1).
P0546: Audit-trail events and payload-preview behaviour are updated where the story touches sensitive actions (CTRL-018).
P0547: No regression into a previously baselined requirement; any baseline change carries a Change Impact Analysis entry with a rollback plan.
P0548: The engine validation gate python -m engine validate Ogma-Library (including the hybrid gate) passes where applicable.
P0549: 5.2Increment-Level Criteria (phase closure)
P0550: All stories of the phase meet the story-level criteria; the phase's specific Definition of Done items from its grand-plan README are met.
P0551: Phase sign-off is recorded (python -m engine signoff), and a baseline snapshot is taken at phase closure.
P0552: 5.3Release-Level Criteria (public build)
P0553: Builds are code-signed; updates verify signatures before install and are reversible (CTRL-012, CTRL-013).
P0554: Formal NFR acceptance evidence exists on W-REF-01/M-REF-01 (Phase 20), the WCAG 2.2 AA audit passes (Phase 21), and the DPIA required actions for any shipped off-device feature are verified.
P0555: The go-live readiness checklist passes with zero open release blockers, including premium icon procurement.
P0556: 6Hybrid Coupling Pointer — Definition of Ready and Definition of Done
P0557: This file is the synchronization point required by the hybrid (Water-Scrum-Fall) methodology. It defines no criteria of its own: the single sources are ../definition-of-ready.md and ../definition-of-done.md. Version 1.0 duplicated summary definitions here; version 2.0 removes the duplication so the criteria cannot diverge.
P0558: 6.1Coupling Rule
P0559: Every story in _registry/baseline-trace.yaml shall trace to at least one baselined identifier of the form FR-, NFR-, or CTRL-*, and shall serve a business goal BG-001 through BG-005. Examples of baselined targets: FR-LIB-001, FR-ADMIN-006, NFR-OGMA-002, CTRL-017. No story enters a sprint unless it meets the Definition of Ready, and no story is done until it meets the Definition of Done, including the grand plan's global items (warnings-as-errors tests, en+fr strings, icon plus aria label per control, ADRs updated, engine hybrid gate).
P0560: 7Sprint Plan — Ogma Library (Remaining Work: Phases 19–23)
P0561: This plan covers only the work that remains after the Phases 00–18 build: grand-plan Phases 19–23 plus the carried V2 debt (Work/Edition, split view, answer mode, command palette and help). The delivered scope is history, not plan; it is recorded in velocity.md and the retrospective. Version 1.0 of this file scheduled Sprints 01–08 over work that was in fact delivered phase-by-phase without sprint ceremonies; those fictional sprints are removed.
P0562: 7.1Capacity Assumptions
P0563: Assumption
P0564: Value
P0565: Sprint length
P0566: 2 weeks
P0567: Team
P0568: 3 contributors: engineering lead (1.0 FTE), AI engineer (0.5 FTE), design/UX with QA/verification duties (0.5 FTE)
P0569: Ceremony and overhead deduction
P0570: 15% of available hours
P0571: Nominal capacity
P0572: 20 story points per sprint
P0573: Forecast basis
P0574: Conservative planning figure; the Phases 00–18 throughput (see velocity.md) was AI-assisted and compressed, and is not extrapolated to hardening, audit, and store-submission work that depends on external parties (reference hardware, store review queues, owner icon procurement)
P0575: Remaining backlog: 116 points across 13 stories (US-010, US-020, US-028, US-050, US-051, US-052, US-053, US-054, US-055, US-056, US-057, US-058, US-059) against 6 × 20 = 120 points of capacity. Sprint-5 is planned 4 points over nominal; US-010 is its named descope candidate.

P0593: US-056 Package, sign, and submit (Velopack, MSIX + Microsoft Store, notarized DMG + Mac App Store, GitHub) — 13 pts (owner: engineering lead)

P0595: Dependencies and risks: code-signing certificates and Apple/Microsoft developer accounts must exist by 2026-08-14; RISK-020 (store rejection) means submission feedback loops can extend past the sprint — submission filing, not store approval, is the sprint's exit criterion. Premium icon procurement (release blocker) must close before this sprint ends.
P0596: 7.6Sprint-5 (2026-09-07 to 2026-09-18) — Phase 23 Beta and Intelligence Completion
P0597: Goal: a public beta opens with the advisor feature-complete. Success: readiness gate passes; answer mode cites local evidence.

P0602: 7.7Sprint-6 (2026-09-21 to 2026-10-02) — Extension Ecosystem

P0607: 7.8Definition of Done Reference
P0608: Every story closes against definition-of-done.md, including the global items: warnings-as-errors build and tests, en+fr strings with pseudolocale pass, icon plus accessible label per new control, ADRs and reference docs updated, and the engine hybrid gate passing.
P0609: 7.9Commitment
P0610: Sprint commitments are made at each sprint planning by the engineering lead, AI engineer, design/UX, and product owner, and recorded in the sprint's planning note. This plan is the owner-sign-off view as of 2026-07-06; re-planning at sprint boundaries supersedes it sprint by sprint.
P0611: 8Delivery Throughput History — Ogma Library
P0612: Version 1.0 seeded velocity figures for sprints that never ran; those numbers are removed. Throughput is therefore reported as code/test evidence by grand-plan phase, not as measured velocity. Forward figures in sprint-plan.md are planning assumptions and require calibration after the first evidenced sprint.

P0670: 9Retrospective — Phases 00–18 Build (held 2026-07-06)
P0671: Version 1.0 contained a fictional "Sprint-01 retrospective" for a sprint that never ran; it is removed. This is a phase-level retrospective of the implementation history through commit 26df98322b7934919ddaba513ac02d9aaa44edbb (299 commits through 2026-07-07), grounded in git and _context/build-status.md. Participant labels identify accountable roles, not evidence of an attended ceremony.
P0672: 9.1Summary
P0673: The repository contains code and automated-test evidence across grand-plan Phases 00–18, including the personal product and LAN/classroom/school stack: 626 test declarations across 120 declaring files, warnings-as-errors, architecture tests around the egress chokepoint, and 11 EF Core migrations ending at Phase18SchoolAdminTables. This does not establish phase or release acceptance: explicit stubs, disabled adapters, and Phases 19–23 hardening, performance, accessibility, packaging, and launch evidence remain open.
P0674: 9.2What Went Well (evidence-cited)
P0675: The phase-gated grand plan held: every phase closed against its own Definition of Done with committed evidence, and phase order (spikes → foundation → modules → classroom) prevented rework — no phase was reopened after closure.
P0676: Architecture discipline paid off measurably: the single egress chokepoint and inward-dependency rules are enforced by OgmaLibrary.Tests.Architecture, so the Phase 18 school AI proxy reused the Phase 12 gateway unchanged.
P0677: The golden-corpus harness (SyntheticCorpusGenerator, ManifestVerifier) caught reader and extraction regressions at commit time rather than at phase gates.
P0678: Risk-first sequencing worked: the 2026-05-30 spike commits retired the .NET 10, PDFium, WebView-bridge, FTS5, and gateway risks before any dependent code existed, and the spike results landed directly in ADR-0001 through ADR-0007.
P0679: 9.3What Went Poorly (evidence-cited)
P0680: Documentation drifted from code: the repo CLAUDE.md and CHANGELOG.md still said "Phases 00–02" while the codebase stood at Phase 18 — the exact stale-status defect _context/build-status.md now exists to counter. Duplicate ADR trees (docs/adrs/ versus docs/architecture/adr/) emerged with one duplicated number.
P0681: Evidence bookkeeping consumed disproportionate effort: on 2026-06-01, roughly 40 of 180 commits were phase-09 evidence-alignment docs commits ("refresh phase 09 …", "align phase09 …"), a signal that the closeout tooling was fighting the process instead of serving it.
P0682: The OGMA0001 hard-coded-string Roslyn analyzer was deferred; the en/fr rule is enforced only by convention plus pseudolocale tests, which cannot catch a string that never reaches a resource file.
P0683: Premium PNG icon procurement from the owner never closed; placeholder and stock flat-color icons remain wired in, and this is a release blocker by policy.
P0684: Dependency hygiene slipped: EF Core 10.0.9 runs on net10.0 with mixed 9.x/10.x Microsoft.Extensions packages — workable, but an undocumented deviation until the ADR-0014 alignment decision is executed.
P0685: Two V2 stubs shipped silently inside otherwise-done phases: split view (FR-READ-012, planned in Phase 15) and answer mode (FR-AI-008, a deliberate NotImplementedException). Deliberate, but the phase-level "Done" claims obscured them until build-status was audited.
P0686: Cross-platform verification was thin: Phase 16 LAN verification was recorded on Windows ("test: record phase 16 windows lan verification"); macOS and Linux received targeted fixes (keychain scoping, secret service) without an equivalent recorded verification pass.
P0687: 9.4Action Items
P0688: A-1 Restore the repo CLAUDE.md and CHANGELOG.md status sections to Phase-18 truth and merge the duplicate ADR trees into one, renumbering the duplicated ADR (owner: engineering lead, due 2026-07-17).
P0689: A-2 Build the OGMA0001 Roslyn analyzer and wire it into the warnings-as-errors build so hard-coded strings fail compilation (owner: engineering lead, due 2026-07-24).
P0690: A-3 Obtain the owner decision and assets for premium PNG icon procurement, or a recorded waiver, before Sprint-4 packaging begins (owner: product owner, due 2026-08-21).
P0691: A-4 Execute the EF Core / Microsoft.Extensions version-alignment decision recorded as ADR-0014: migrate or formally accept the 9.x-on-net10.0 mix (owner: engineering lead, due 2026-07-31).
P0692: A-5 Record a macOS and Linux LAN/classroom verification pass equivalent to the Phase 16 Windows evidence (owner: QA/verification, due 2026-08-07).
P0693: A-6 Replace per-commit evidence-alignment docs churn with a single per-phase closeout script that regenerates evidence idempotently (owner: engineering lead, due 2026-08-07).
P0694: A-7 Refresh _registry/baseline-trace.yaml to the v2.0 story set (US-001 through US-059) so the hybrid gate traces against the current stories (owner: QA/verification, due 2026-07-17).
P0695: 9.5Follow-Up
P0696: Actions are reviewed at each forward sprint review, starting Sprint-1 (2026-07-24). An action open past its due date is escalated to the product owner at the next phase gate. Recurring-theme tracking starts with this retrospective as the baseline entry; the dominant theme recorded is "documentation and evidence lag code under compressed delivery".

P0018: 2Value, Buyers, and Measurable Outcomes
P0019: Segment
P0020: Job
P0021: Proposed proof metric
P0022: Current evidence

P0024: Turn unmanaged PDFs into a durable searchable library
P0025: Median time to first useful search; metadata repair completion; weekly retained libraries
P0026: NOT ASSESSED
P0027: Privacy-sensitive readers
P0028: Use AI only with visible, controllable payloads
P0029: Preview-to-consent rate; local-tier use; erasure success
P0030: Code/test evidence only
P0031: Schools and libraries
P0032: Share a governed local collection on a LAN
P0033: Setup time; concurrent students; support tickets; privacy incidents; renewal intent
P0034: NOT ASSESSED; deployment NO-GO
P0035: The differentiator is a proposed bundle of local ownership, structured reading, transparent AI, and optional classroom operation. A finite competitor screen cannot prove uniqueness. Product-market fit requires at least 12 documented target-user interviews across personal and institutional segments, 5 observed workflow tests, and a paid or signed-pilot signal before final pricing.
P0036: 3Commercial Model and Packaging Hypotheses
P0037: Offer
P0038: Hypothesis

P0048: NOT APPROVED

P0050: Institution/host plus governed seat band
P0051: Admin roles, student privacy pack, support SLA, deployment assistance, seat reconciliation

No “lifetime” claim is permitted without a precise product/version and support definition.
P0054: 4Economics, Accounting, and Control Model
P0055: No TAM, revenue forecast, margin, CAC, LTV, or payback figure is presented because input evidence was not supplied. Before approval, the owner and finance controller shall complete:
P0056: unit economics by personal, update-plan, and education offer;
P0057: store/direct gross-to-net bridge: gross sale, VAT/sales tax, store fee, refunds/chargebacks, net settlement, and FX;
P0058: accounting policy for one-time licences, update obligations, support, education services, and deferred revenue where obligations span periods;
P0059: cash/store/mobile settlement reconciliation and exception ownership;
P0060: AI usage ledger reconciliation: provider invoice, school quota, retry/failed-call treatment, and customer-facing overage rule; and
P0061: refund, cancellation, credit-note, tax-document, bad-debt, and access-revocation workflows with immutable audit evidence.
P0062: Commercial approval requires a contribution-margin floor chosen by the owner, measured support effort, and a sensitivity table for channel fees, refund rate, provider cost, and FX. Until those inputs exist, finance status is Conditional / NOT PRODUCTION APPROVED.

P0022: 1.2Scope Note
P0023: Ogma Library is a local-first desktop application for Windows and macOS with an opt-in LAN host mode for classrooms. It is not a hosted multi-tenant service. This document therefore covers the operations a desktop product actually owns: release engineering across the Dev, Alpha, Beta, and Stable channels (ADR-0009); the signed auto-update trust chain; the build/sign/notarize pipeline; opt-in, local-first telemetry; the release-hosting and code-signing supply chain; security incident response for signing keys and the update feed; and — new in version 2.0 — the operational runbook for a school running the LAN host.
P0024: 1.3Honesty Baseline (read before any checklist in this phase)
P0025: Version 2.0 exists to correct a defect in v1.0: the v1.0 go-live checklist was presented pre-checked, asserting that gates, rollback drills, signing, and telemetry verification had all passed when none of that infrastructure exists. That was fiction and it is withdrawn. In version 2.0:
P0026: Every checklist item is unchecked unless _context/build-status.md or a named repository artifact proves it, and each open item names an owner, an evidence pointer, and a target phase.
P0027: Distribution, packaging, signing, the release host, and the telemetry sink are designed here but not built — the repository contains no packaging configuration (Phase 22 scope) and no telemetry sink (Phase 23 scope). Sections describing them are written as executable plans and are explicitly marked Planned.
P0028: What does exist and is verifiable today: the application itself (Phases 00–18 implemented), 800 executed automated cases with 0 failures in the 2026-08-13 canonical Release run (see ../05-testing-documentation/test-completion-report.md), the CI pipeline on Windows + macOS, and the governance documents this phase mirrors.
P0029: This document is the intellectual property of Chwezi Core Systems. No part may be reproduced or disclosed without written permission.
P0030: \newpage
P0031: 2Release and Update Infrastructure
P0032: Ogma Library runs on the user's device; the only remote infrastructure the product owns is the release-hosting and code-signing supply chain plus an opt-in crash-report sink. No user library content, catalogue data, or reading state is ever stored on an Ogma Library server. This section inventories that infrastructure with an explicit status per component — most of it is planned, not provisioned.

P0074: 2.22. Update Trust Chain (design ratified; verification lands at Phase 22)
P0075: Per ADR-0009 and CTRL-OGMA-012/013:
P0076: CI signs each Windows build (Authenticode) and signs + notarizes each macOS build (Developer ID), requesting signing operations from the key-custody service over an authenticated session — key material never leaves the store, and every signing operation is logged with the requesting build identifier.
P0077: The Velopack feed descriptor is itself signed, so descriptor verification is independent of transport.
P0078: The installed application verifies descriptor signature, then package signature, before applying an update; a failure discards the package and writes a local AuditEvents record.
P0079: Signing material rotates on a fixed schedule and immediately on suspected compromise (runbook: signing-certificate expiry; malicious-update entry).
P0080: 2.33. Crash-Report Sink Design (Planned — Phase 23)
P0081: Accepts data only from users who enabled opt-in telemetry. Payload minimized at source: anonymous install identifier, application version, OS version, exception type + stack signature, aggregate counters. Never: library content, file paths or names, catalogue metadata, search queries, AI history. The same payload-preview and privacy-tier rules that govern AI egress govern this surface. The default-off behaviour is verifiable today by construction — no telemetry code exists — and will be re-verified by an explicit default-off egress test when the surface is built.
P0082: 2.44. Classroom/LAN Infrastructure (school-owned, product-supported)
P0083: The LAN host is implemented in the product (Phase 16–18 code: KestrelHostModeListener, mDNS advertisement, TLS provisioning, trust store) and runs on school-owned hardware — it is the school's infrastructure, operated with the LAN-host runbook in runbook.md. Chwezi Core Systems hosts nothing for a school; the school's AI keys live in the host machine's OS credential store (FR-ADMIN-004) and its audit log is local and exportable (FR-LAN-010).
P0084: 2.55. Incident-Response Flow
P0085: The detect → triage → contain → eradicate → recover → review flow below is authoritative for the two highest-severity infrastructure incidents — signing-key compromise and a malicious-update report. Per-incident operational steps live in runbook.md. The flow is a written procedure; the first tabletop rehearsal is a Phase 23 exit criterion (go-live register Section 4).
P0086: flowchart TD    A[Detect] --> B[Triage]    B --> C[Contain]    C --> D[Eradicate]    D --> E[Recover]    E --> F[Review]    A -->|Sources: crash-report sink anomaly,\nmalicious-update report,\nkey-store access alert| B    B -->|Classify severity,\nassign incident owner| C    C -->|Pull the affected feed descriptor,\nsuspend publishing,\nrevoke or freeze signing key| D    D -->|Rotate signing material,\nrebuild from a clean runner,\nremove the bad package| E    E -->|Publish a signed corrective release,\nverify update success,\nrestore the channel| F    F -->|Post-incident review,\nupdate this flow and the runbook,\nrecord lessons| G[Closed]
P0087: The flow is reviewed after every invocation and at each release-gate sign-off so the diagram and runbook stay consistent with the live infrastructure.
P0088: \newpage
P0089: 3Release, Build, Sign, and Distribute Guide
P0090: Implementation status: PLANNED. This guide is the executable plan for Phase 22 ("Packaging, Signing & Store Submission", docs/plans/grand-plan/phase-22/README.md, status: not started). The distribution design is ratified — ADR-0009 (docs/adrs/0009-velopack-msix-dmg.md, ratified 2026-05-30) — and Velopack 1.0.1 was proven to restore/build on net10.0 in spike S1. No packaging configuration exists in the repository today: no vpk invocation, no MSIX manifest, no notarization profile, no release workflow beyond CI build-and-test. Every command below is the plan to be executed and verified at Phase 22, not a record of something that has run.
P0091: 3.11. Distribution Targets (ADR-0009)
P0092: One pipeline produces four coordinated distribution targets:
P0093: Target
P0094: Format
P0095: Channel role
P0096: Tier (Phase 22 plan)
P0097: Velopack direct feed
P0098: Full + delta packages per channel (Dev/Alpha/Beta/Stable), Windows and macOS
P0099: Team-controlled auto-update path; signature-verified descriptor and packages
P0100: MVP
P0101: GitHub Releases
P0102: Velopack full installers + checksums attached to tagged releases
P0103: Public download of record; beta distribution alongside the Beta feed
P0104: MVP
P0105: Microsoft Store

P0107: Store discovery and enterprise policy deployment on Windows
P0108: V1

P0110: Notarized, Developer ID-signed DMG for direct; MAS build for the store
P0111: macOS distribution meeting Gatekeeper expectations
P0112: V1 (DMG) / Final (MAS)
P0113: 3.22. Channel Model
P0114: Four Velopack feed paths separate stability tiers. A build is promoted, never rebuilt — promotion re-points the next channel's feed at the already-signed artifact, which is why Deterministic=true builds and signed packages matter:
P0115: Dev — every merge to develop publishes a Dev build (continuous, no change window).
P0116: Alpha — promotion of a Dev build that passed smoke verification on Windows and macOS.
P0117: Beta — promotion of an Alpha build after every release gate G1–G8 passes formally (Test Strategy Section 7 — none has passed formally yet).
P0118: Stable — promotion of a Beta build that completed its soak window (≥ 2 weeks, Phase 23 plan) without a blocking defect.
P0119: 3.33. Planned Pipeline (to be implemented and drilled at Phase 22)
P0120: Steps 1–10 constitute the release procedure; each acquires its verification evidence at Phase 22 before first use.
P0121: Tag the release commit on release/<semver>: git tag -s v<semver> (signed tag; main merge rules per docs/governance/BRANCH-STRATEGY.md). The tag is the single release coordinate on every artifact and gate record.
P0122: CI builds Release configuration from the tag on both runners: dotnet publish src/OgmaLibrary.App -c Release -r win-x64 and -r osx-arm64 (plus osx-x64 if the owner ratifies Intel-mac support; open decision for Phase 22).
P0123: Run the full verification suite; a channel release proceeds only when the gates applicable to that channel pass and the run is recorded.
P0124: Windows signing: sign the published binaries with Authenticode via the key-custody service (Section 4 of infrastructure.md); key material never lands on the runner disk.
P0125: Velopack packaging (both platforms): vpk pack --packId OgmaLibrary --packVersion <semver> --packDir <publish-dir> --channel <channel> producing full + delta packages and the feed descriptor; then vpk upload (or an equivalent copy step) to the channel's feed path on the release host. Exact vpk argument set is fixed when Phase 22 lands the tooling version.
P0126: macOS: sign with Developer ID (codesign --deep --options runtime), notarize (xcrun notarytool submit <artifact> --wait), staple (xcrun stapler staple), then produce the DMG.
P0127: MSIX (Stable only): produce the MSIX from the publish output with a manifest to be authored in Phase 22 (Package.appxmanifest), sign, validate with the Windows App Certification Kit, submit via Partner Center.
P0128: Sign the Velopack feed descriptor itself so descriptor verification is independent of transport (CTRL-OGMA-012/013).
P0129: Publish: feed paths for the channel + GitHub Release with checksums.
P0130: Record the release in the release log: version, channel, commit, signing identifiers, gate-run references.
P0131: 3.44. Update Trust Chain (design; verification oracles land with Phase 22 tests)
P0132: The installed application fetches the channel descriptor over an integrity-checked connection.
P0133: It verifies the descriptor signature, then the package signature, before applying anything.
P0134: A package failing either check is discarded and the failure is recorded in the local AuditEvents table.
P0135: Phase 22 test oracle: a deliberately tampered descriptor and a tampered package must both be rejected on both platforms (Test Strategy Section 9.4).
P0136: 3.55. Change Window and Freeze Policy (policy — effective from first Beta)
P0137: Routine Beta/Stable releases publish Tuesday or Thursday, 09:00–13:00 East Africa Time, at the start of the window so the release owner watches the update-success signal for the remainder.
P0138: Code freeze begins 24 hours before a scheduled Stable window; only release-blocking fixes merge during freeze.
P0139: Beta/Stable testers receive a release notice ≥ 24 hours ahead: version, headline changes, and whether a database migration is included.
P0140: Emergency security releases may publish out-of-window through the full pipeline, with the justification recorded in the release log.
P0141: 3.66. Database Migration Safety (implemented in-app; release procedure planned)
P0142: The application side exists and is tested: migrations apply transactionally with a backup-before-apply snapshot of the catalogue of record, verified by Catalogue/MigrationTests.cs across the 10 EF Core migrations (latest Phase18SchoolAdminTables). The release-side rules:
P0143: A release that includes a migration says so in its release notice.
P0144: The pre-migration backup is retained until the migrated version has launched successfully at least once, so a downgrade path exists.
P0145: 3.77. Rollback Procedure (planned; drilled against a staging feed before first Beta — a go-live blocker)
P0146: Detect the regression (update-success signal, crash signal, or user report); open an incident per runbook.md.
P0147: Freeze publishing on the affected channel.
P0148: Re-point the channel feed descriptor at the previous known-good signed release. The previous 2 Stable releases' full packages and descriptors are always retained for exactly this purpose.
P0149: Publish the previous version as current so auto-update pulls the older build forward.
P0150: If the defective update migrated the database, the release notice and in-app message direct users to the backup-restore steps in the runbook's corrupted-database entry before downgrading.
P0151: Confirm recovery via the update-success and crash-free signals; record the rollback and feed the cause into the post-incident review.
P0152: Status: this procedure has never been executed. The go-live readiness register carries "rollback drill executed against a staging feed" as an unchecked blocker with owner and target phase (Phase 22).
P0153: \newpage
P0154: 4Telemetry and Service Levels
P0155: Implementation status: definitions binding now; measurement surface Planned — Phase 23. Ogma Library currently transmits no telemetry of any kind because no telemetry code exists. When the opt-in surface is built (Phase 23), these definitions govern it; until then the SLO table is a commitment, not a measurement.
P0156: 4.11. Telemetry Model — Opt-In, Local-First
P0157: Telemetry is opt-in; the default state collects and transmits nothing off-device. The product is fully functional with telemetry off, forever.
P0158: Payload is minimized at source: anonymous install identifier, application version, OS version, exception type + stack signature, aggregate event counters. Never library content, file paths/names, catalogue metadata, search queries, or AI history.
P0159: The user can inspect what would be sent (the same payload-preview pattern the AI gateway uses), disable at any time, and request erasure of previously sent reports (NFR-PROD-014 pattern applied to telemetry).
P0160: Local health signals (job failures, crash markers, audit events in AuditEvents) remain on-device regardless of the telemetry setting; the LAN-host audit log is local to the school.
P0161: 4.22. Definitions
P0162: SLI — a measured quantity describing one aspect of service health.
P0163: SLO — the target an SLI must meet over a stated window. All objectives here are internal SLOs over a rolling 30-day window; Ogma Library makes no contractual SLA to end users. The single provider-side SLA is the release host/CDN's own commitment, tracked under its contract.
P0164: Error budget — the share of events permitted to miss the objective in the window; exhaustion freezes non-essential releases on the affected channel and triggers runbook escalation.
P0165: 4.33. Service-Level Objectives (binding from first public Beta)
P0166: SLI
P0167: Definition
P0168: SLO
P0169: Error budget
P0170: Update-success rate
P0171: Successful update applications ÷ update attempts reported by consenting clients; success = signature verification passes and the new version launches once
P0172: ≥ 99.0%
P0173: 1.0% of attempts
P0174: Crash-free session rate
P0175: Sessions ending without an unhandled-exception termination ÷ total sessions from consenting clients
P0176: ≥ 99.5%
P0177: 0.5% of sessions
P0178: Release-host availability
P0179: Successful feed/package responses ÷ total requests (synthetic probes + consenting-client outcomes)
P0180: ≥ 99.9% (≈ 43 min/30 days)
P0181: 0.1% of requests
P0182: Median update download time
P0183: Median wall-clock time to download a delta package, normalized to a reference 25 Mbps connection
P0184: ≤ 8 s at the median
P0185: Binds at the median; the slower half is not budgeted
P0186: Notes per indicator:
P0187: A signature-verification failure counts against update-success and raises the malicious-update runbook path — it is never just a statistic.
P0188: Crash-free 99.5% is the productivity-domain target behind NFR-PROD-006; it is unmeasurable until the Phase 23 opt-in surface exists, which is exactly why NFR-PROD-006 carries "no evidence possible today" in the test strategy.
P0189: Median download time isolates delta-packaging regressions from user link speed; a breach is investigated in the packaging pipeline, not blamed on networks.
P0190: 4.44. Classroom/LAN Host Signals (school-local, never transmitted)
P0191: The LAN host exposes operational signals to the school administrator only, on the host machine: active session count and per-session activity (FR-LAN-007), host audit log of connections/streams/denials (FR-LAN-010), AI usage/cost/quota utilization per class and student (FR-ADMIN-010). These are dashboards over local data (SchoolUsageDashboardServiceTests verifies the figures), not telemetry — nothing leaves the school.
P0192: 4.55. Reporting and Review
P0193: The four SLIs are reviewed at each release-gate sign-off and at each 30-day window close, from first Beta onward.
P0194: A window with an exhausted budget is recorded; cause analysed in the post-incident review; corrective action tracked to closure before the next Stable change window.
P0195: SLO instrumentation itself is a go-live blocker: the readiness register carries it unchecked with owner and Phase 23 target until the measurement surface exists and has produced its first honest window.
P0196: \newpage
P0197: 5Operational Runbook
P0198: This runbook gives a responder a deterministic procedure per incident. Two operating contexts exist: release operations (Chwezi Core Systems' own supply chain — feeds, signing, telemetry) and, new in version 2.0, LAN-host operations (a school running the classroom host on its own hardware). Release-operations entries that depend on the update feed or telemetry are executable only once Phase 22–23 infrastructure exists; they are the procedures that will be drilled at Phase 23. The LAN-host entries are executable today because the host features are implemented (Phases 16–18).
P0199: 5.11. Incident Severity
P0200: Severity
P0201: Definition
P0202: Initial response time
P0203: SEV-1
P0204: Signing-key compromise; malicious-update report; an update that crashes on launch for a population; school AI key exposure
P0205: 15 minutes
P0206: SEV-2
P0207: Crash spike above error budget; failed-update rate breaching its SLO on a live channel; LAN host serving unpublished content
P0208: 1 hour
P0209: SEV-3
P0210: Single user's corrupted database; non-blocking update failure; single classroom client fault
P0211: 1 business day
P0212: 5.22. Release Operations
P0213: 5.2.12.1 Failed Update (executable from first Beta)
P0214: Classify: signature-verification rejection, download failure, or post-install launch failure (crash-sink signatures + release-host logs).
P0215: Signature rejection of a legitimate package → treat as SEV-1 and jump to 2.4 (a verification failure may indicate a tampered feed).
P0216: Post-install launch failure → freeze the channel, execute the rollback procedure (deployment guide Section 7).
P0217: Release host failing to serve → escalate to the CDN provider under its agreement.
P0218: Confirm recovery: update-success SLI returns above 99.0%.
P0219: 5.2.22.2 Corrupted Local Database (executable today — procedure is app-level)
P0220: User closes the application so no process holds the database file.
P0221: Restore the most recent timestamped backup of the catalogue of record and sidecar index (created by the backup-before-apply rule; the mechanism is tested by Catalogue/MigrationTests.cs).
P0222: No backup → rebuild derived state: re-scan the library root (files remain the user's files, FR-LIB principles) and rebuild the search index from source (IndexManagerService rebuild path, FR-SEARCH-006). User-authored data inside the database cannot be rebuilt — which is why the backup rule exists.
P0223: If corruption followed an update, escalate to 2.1 and consider a channel rollback.
P0224: 5.2.32.3 Crash Spike (executable from first Beta; needs telemetry)
P0225: Declare SEV-2; assign an incident owner.
P0226: Group crash signatures; correlate the dominant fault with the latest release on the channel.
P0227: Release-correlated → freeze channel, roll back. Environment-specific → targeted fix at the next change window.
P0228: Confirm: crash-free SLI back above 99.5%.
P0229: 5.2.42.4 Malicious-Update Report / Signing-Key Compromise (SEV-1)
P0230: Follow the incident-response flow in infrastructure.md Section 5:
P0231: Contain: suspend publishing; pull the affected feed descriptor; freeze or revoke the signing key.
P0232: Eradicate: rotate signing material; rebuild from a clean runner; remove the bad package.
P0233: Recover: publish a signed corrective release; re-point the channel; verify update-success recovery.
P0234: Review: post-incident review; update this runbook and the flow diagram.
P0235: First tabletop rehearsal of this entry is a Phase 23 exit criterion — it has not been drilled.
P0236: 5.2.52.5 Signing-Certificate Expiry
P0237: Renewal is scheduled ≥ 30 days ahead of expiry (monitored alert once key custody exists, Phase 22).
P0238: Install the renewed certificate into the key store; re-run a Dev build to confirm signing and notarization.
P0239: Already expired with releases blocked → SEV-1; escalate per Section 4.
P0240: 5.33. LAN-Host Operations (school administrator; executable today)
P0241: These procedures operate the implemented classroom host. The admin surfaces referenced are the host-sharing panel (HostSharingViewModel in OgmaLibrary.App) and the school-admin dashboard; behaviours cited are covered by the tests named in the deterministic-check catalogue (DC-LAN, DC-ADMIN).
P0242: 5.3.13.1 Enable / Disable Host Mode
P0243: Enable: open the sharing panel → start hosting → confirm the explicit start-confirmation prompt (host mode is opt-in and audited, FR-LAN-001). Verify: clients can discover the host (mDNS) or join by address; the join code/QR displays; the audit log records the start event.
P0244: Disable: stop hosting from the same panel. Verify: the listener stops (no inbound port); connected clients degrade gracefully to their local libraries (FR-LAN-008, FR-CLIENT-011) and re-sync on the next start.
P0245: Publishing scope check after any enable: only admin-published folders/shelves are enumerable (FR-LAN-009). If unpublished content is reachable, treat as SEV-2, disable host mode immediately, and export the audit log before investigating.
P0246: 5.3.23.2 TLS Certificate Rotation (host)
P0247: Rotate the host certificate from the sharing panel (provisioning is automatic — LanHostCertificateProvisioner).
P0248: Expect every client to see a fingerprint change on next connection: trust-on-first-use means clients must refuse the new fingerprint until re-trusted (FR-LAN-004; HostTrustServiceTests verifies refusal).
P0249: Communicate the new fingerprint to the class through an out-of-band channel (displayed on the host screen); students re-trust after comparing fingerprints.
P0250: Rotate immediately (not on schedule) if the host machine was compromised or the certificate's private key may have leaked; then also revoke all sessions (3.3).
P0251: 5.3.33.3 Session and Enrollment Revocation
P0252: Revoke a session: sharing panel → sessions list → revoke. The revoked session's next request is rejected; other sessions are unaffected (FR-LAN-007).
P0253: Revoke an enrollment (student/teacher profile): admin panel → profiles → revoke. Takes effect at next connection; the student keeps local notes but loses host access (FR-CLIENT-009).
P0254: After any suspected credential sharing: revoke the affected profile, re-issue a join code, and export the host audit log (FR-LAN-010) for the period in question.
P0255: 5.3.43.4 School AI Key Rotation / Exposure (SEV-1 if exposed)
P0256: The school AI key lives only in the host machine's OS credential store and is never transmitted to clients (FR-ADMIN-004; AiProxyEndpointHandlerTests asserts no key bytes in client payloads).
P0257: Routine rotation: admin panel → school AI settings → delete the stored key → save the new key → run the key test action. Verify a student query succeeds through the host gateway afterward.
P0258: Exposure response: revoke the key at the AI provider first, then rotate in-app; review the AI usage dashboard (FR-ADMIN-010) for anomalous spend during the exposure window; purge institution AI history if student queries may have been exfiltrated (FR-ADMIN-013).
P0259: Quota sanity check after rotation: per-student and per-class quotas and rate limits still enforce (FR-ADMIN-008/009).
P0260: 5.3.53.5 Host Machine Failure
P0261: Clients continue reading from offline caches and local state — nothing is lost by design (FR-LAN-008, FR-CLIENT-005/011).
P0262: Restore the host: reinstall Ogma Library, restore the host catalogue from the school's backup of the library folder + catalogue database, re-enable host mode, re-provision the certificate (clients re-trust per 3.2).
P0263: Client reading state re-syncs on reconnect with per-field latest-writer-wins and no silent loss (FR-CLIENT-006).

P0293: 6Public-Beta Go-Live Readiness Register
P0294: This register is the go/no-go gate for opening the public Beta channel. Version 2.0 replaces the v1.0 checklist, which was published pre-checked — asserting passed gates, executed rollback drills, verified signing, and instrumented SLOs when none of that infrastructure exists. That presentation was fictional and is withdrawn. The rule in force now:
P0295: An item is checked only when a named evidence artifact proves it. Every unchecked item carries an owner, an evidence pointer (what will prove it), and a target phase. Current truth source: _context/build-status.md and commit 26df98322b7934919ddaba513ac02d9aaa44edbb, verified 2026-08-13.
P0296: Overall recommendation as of 2026-08-13: NO-GO for public beta or production. The repository has broad code/test evidence, but explicit capability stubs and the Phase 19–23 security, performance, accessibility, packaging, operational, legal, design, and commercial gates remain open.

P0298: Status vocabulary: automated oracle green = the gate's pass oracle runs and passes in the test suite today; formal acceptance = a recorded gate run on reference hardware or against a release-candidate build, which is what "passed" means. Detail per gate: Test Strategy Section 7.

P0019: Standard
P0020: BS ISO/IEC/IEEE 29119-3 (test case and procedure specification alignment)
P0021: This catalogue enumerates the deterministic checks that verify the Ogma Library functional-requirement groups, per ISO/IEC/IEEE 29119-3: each check states a stimulus, a deterministic oracle (a pass/fail criterion requiring no judgement), its automation status, and the implementing test class in C:\wamp64\www\Ogma-Library where one exists. Check IDs are stable (DC-<MODULE>-NNN) and are the unit of traceability between requirements (SRS Section 3.2), the test strategy coverage matrix (Section 8), and recorded runs (test-completion-report.md).
P0022: Automation status values: Automated (runs in the suite today), Planned-P19/P20/P21/P22/P23 (owned by that unbuilt phase), Manual (scripted human procedure at a release gate).

P0555: 1.11Catalogue Governance
P0556: A new automated test family must register its checks here in the same change; a check without an implementing class must carry a Planned-phase owner.
P0557: Check IDs are never reused; a retired check is marked withdrawn with a reason.
P0558: The recorded pass/fail state of the automated checks is the test-run record in test-completion-report.md; this catalogue defines what is checked, the report records whether it passed on a date.
P0560: Kaizen refresh - 2026-09-10
P0561: Refresh status: Applied local remediation and current-source updates. Visual/render/browser inspection was skipped at the requester's direction and is NOT ASSESSED.
P0562: Source: docs/references/Ogma-Library_DeterministicChecks_v2.1_2026-08-13.docx; repository head at evidence capture: e2be72fb332e
P0563: Current-source requirements, design, and test update
P0564: Specify PDF containment, page-count/navigation behaviour, malformed-document handling, interrupted-load recovery, search failure, identity, and metadata boundaries.
P0565: Specify reproducible build inputs, signing/notarisation, update/rollback, supported OS/runtime matrix, hashes, and release ownership.
P0566: Use WCAG 2.2 test oracles for reader keyboard/focus/status/reflow/error-recovery requirements; automated tests do not close physical/platform gates.
P0567: Design-system decision
P0568: Preserve the source document's existing typography, spacing, colour, and table styles. No new primary typeface is introduced in this no-visual-QA cycle; no visual quality claim is made.
P0569: Verification and release posture
P0570: Current automated baseline: 42 architecture, 955 core, and 163 UI tests (1,160 total); shelf3d typecheck/build/performance baseline was green.
P0571: Keep release, PDF containment, signing, platform/accessibility, and reviewer evidence open until directly observed.
P0572: Operational control
P0573: Do not promote on automated green tests alone; require artifact hashes, signing evidence, rollback evidence, and supported-runtime proof.
