P0024: Classification
P0025: Confidential — Internal Use Only
P0026: Baseline note: version 2.0 supersedes the v0.1 draft of 2026-05-30. Where v0.1 prescribed intended standards, version 2.0 documents the standards as enforced in the codebase at C:\wamp64\www\Ogma-Library (verified 2026-07-06 against _context/build-status.md): Directory.Build.props at the solution root, NetArchTest-enforced layering in tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests.Architecture/ArchitectureTests.cs, the governance rules in docs/governance/, and the single composition root in src/OgmaLibrary.App/CompositionRoot.cs. Every rule in this document cites the repository file that enforces it or is explicitly marked as convention-only.
P0027: This document is the intellectual property of Chwezi Core Systems. No part may be reproduced or disclosed without written permission.
P0028: \newpage
P0029: 2Coding Standards for the Ogma Library Codebase
P0030: These standards are binding on every project in OgmaLibrary.sln (7 production projects under src/, 3 test projects under tests/). Version 2.0 states each rule together with the mechanism that enforces it in the repository at C:\wamp64\www\Ogma-Library. A rule with no automated enforcement is marked convention-only so a reviewer knows it must be checked by hand. A pull request that violates a rule in this document is not ready to merge.
P0031: 2.11. Solution-Wide Build Properties (Directory.Build.props)
P0032: The authoritative build configuration is Directory.Build.props at the solution root. It applies to every project under src/ and tests/; the throwaway spikes under spikes/ are shielded by their own spikes/Directory.Build.props. The live file sets:
P0033: <PropertyGroup>  <TargetFramework>net10.0</TargetFramework>  <LangVersion>latest</LangVersion>  <Nullable>enable</Nullable>  <ImplicitUsings>enable</ImplicitUsings>  <TreatWarningsAsErrors>true</TreatWarningsAsErrors>  <EnforceCodeStyleInBuild>true</EnforceCodeStyleInBuild>  <AnalysisLevel>latest-recommended</AnalysisLevel>  <GenerateDocumentationFile>true</GenerateDocumentationFile>  <Deterministic>true</Deterministic>  <ManagePackageVersionsCentrally>false</ManagePackageVersionsCentrally>  <RootNamespace>OgmaLibrary.$(MSBuildProjectName.Replace('OgmaLibrary.', ''))</RootNamespace></PropertyGroup>
P0034: Consequences of these settings, in order of day-to-day impact:
P0035: Warnings are errors everywhere. Any compiler warning, analyzer warning, or NuGet audit warning fails the build. Do not lower TreatWarningsAsErrors per project; fix the cause or record a scoped, owner-approved, expiring waiver. The July 2026 NU1903 finding is closed in the current baseline; the 2026-08-13 package audit reported no vulnerable packages.
P0036: XML documentation is required on every public member of OgmaLibrary.Domain, OgmaLibrary.Application, OgmaLibrary.Infrastructure, OgmaLibrary.Reader, OgmaLibrary.Bookshelf3D, and OgmaLibrary.Workers (missing docs raise CS1591, which is an error). A conditional <NoWarn> block in Directory.Build.props exempts only the test projects and OgmaLibrary.App, because the Avalonia shell and tests are not part of the documented public library surface.
P0037: Deterministic builds (Deterministic=true) so a given commit always produces byte-identical assemblies — a precondition for the ADR-0009 promote-without-rebuild release model.
P0038: Nullable reference types are on solution-wide. Do not silence a nullable warning with the null-forgiving operator (!); either guard the value or model it as nullable. Validate arguments at the boundary with ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(value).
P0039: Root namespace is derived from the project name (OgmaLibrary.<Project>); do not override it per project.
P0040: Package sources are pinned by nuget.config at the repo root, which opens with <clear /> to drop machine-level sources (a broken local Telerik source corrupted restores during the Phase 01 spikes — see spikes/RESULTS.md, S1). Never add a package source in a project file.
P0041: 2.22. Layering: Clean/Onion Rules Enforced by NetArchTest
P0042: The Clean/Onion dependency direction is not a diagram — it is executable. tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests.Architecture/ArchitectureTests.cs (NetArchTest.Rules 1.3.2) fails the build when a dependency edge points outward. The binding rules and the test that enforces each:
P0043: Rule
P0044: Enforcing test
P0045: OgmaLibrary.Domain references no other project and no infrastructure package
P0046: Architecture_DomainProject_HasNoOutwardDependencies
P0047: Only OgmaLibrary.Infrastructure may use HttpClient — the single HTTP-egress chokepoint
P0048: Architecture_OnlyInfrastructureUsesHttpClient
P0049: Only OgmaLibrary.App binds interfaces to implementations (single composition root)
P0050: Architecture_OnlyAppBindsImplementations
P0051: Only OgmaLibrary.Infrastructure uses Entity Framework Core
P0052: Architecture_OnlyInfrastructureUsesEntityFrameworkCore
P0053: OgmaLibrary.Workers never depends on the App project
P0054: Architecture_WorkersProject_HasNoDependencyOnAppProject
P0055: AI provider HttpClient use stays inside adapter namespaces
P0056: Architecture_AiProviderHttpClients_StayInAdapterNamespaces
P0057: The Reader context stays independent of Infrastructure, Search, and AI
P0058: Architecture_Reader_DoesNotDependOnInfrastructure and siblings
P0059: The advisor reaches models only through the AI gateway
P0060: Architecture_AdvisorService_UsesOnlyAiGateway
P0061: Standalone (non-host) mode opens no inbound listener
P0062: ArchTests_StandaloneMode_HasNoOpenListener
P0063: The LAN host has no AI-provider or credential-store dependency
P0064: ArchTests_LanHost_HasNoAiProviderDependency, ArchTests_LanHost_HasNoCredentialStoreOrWorkerDependency
P0065: The classroom client cannot write to the host catalogue directly
P0066: ArchTests_ClassroomClient_HasNoDirectCatalogueWriteDependency
P0067: When you add a project reference or a package that touches I/O, run the architecture project first:
P0068: dotnet test tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests.Architecture --nologo
P0069: A red architecture test means the design is wrong, not the test. Change the code; do not loosen the rule without an ADR amendment and a Change Impact Analysis entry (docs/governance/CIA-WORKFLOW.md).
P0070: 2.33. Single Composition Root
P0071: src/OgmaLibrary.App/CompositionRoot.cs is the only place a concrete implementation is bound to an interface (43 service registrations as of 2026-07-06). Enforced by Architecture_OnlyAppBindsImplementations. Practical rules:
P0072: A view model receives query/service interfaces via constructor injection; it never news up an EF Core context, an HttpClient, or a PDF renderer.
P0073: Adding a service means: declare the interface in OgmaLibrary.Application (or Domain for domain services), implement it in the owning context project, register it in CompositionRoot.cs.
P0074: AI is off by default: the composition root binds AiDisabledProvider unless the user has configured a provider, satisfying FR-AI-001.
P0075: 2.44. Internationalization: No Hard-Coded User-Facing Strings
P0076: Every user-facing string is externalized to the .resx resources under src/OgmaLibrary.App/Assets/Strings/ (paired *.en.resx and *.fr.resx files per surface, for example annotations.en.resx / annotations.fr.resx) and resolved through the localization service (src/OgmaLibrary.Infrastructure/Localization/InMemoryLocalizationService.cs).
P0077: Enforcement status — be precise about this: the planned OGMA0001 Roslyn analyzer that would flag a hard-coded string literal at compile time is not built (known open item, _context/build-status.md). Until it exists the rule is enforced two ways:
P0078: Convention at code review — a reviewer rejects any string literal rendered to the user.
P0079: Pseudolocale tests — UI tests (for example in tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests.Ui/ReaderViewRenderTests.cs and SearchViewModelTests.cs) render surfaces under a pseudolocale; a hard-coded string surfaces untranslated and fails the assertion.
P0080: The CIA checklist item 4 (docs/governance/CIA-WORKFLOW.md) requires every PR that adds user strings to externalize them in en + fr and pass the pseudolocale tests.
P0081: 2.55. Naming Conventions
P0082: Namespaces, types, methods, properties, events: PascalCase. Root namespace OgmaLibrary, extended per project (Section 1).
P0083: Locals and parameters: camelCase. Private fields: _camelCase.
P0084: Constants: PascalCase, never SCREAMING_CASE.
P0085: Interfaces carry the I prefix (IAiProvider, ICatalogueReadModel); async methods carry the Async suffix; the cancellation parameter is named cancellationToken.
P0086: Use full glossary terms in identifiers (ReadingProgress, never RdProg). The glossary of record is _context/glossary.md in the project workspace.
P0087: Formatting is governed by .editorconfig at the repo root plus EnforceCodeStyleInBuild=true; run dotnet format OgmaLibrary.sln --verify-no-changes before every commit (CI runs the same check on both Windows and macOS per .github/workflows/ci.yml).
P0088: 2.66. Records versus Classes
P0089: Record: immutable data carriers — value objects, domain events, command/query messages, the scene model sent over the 3D bridge (tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests/Shelf3D/BridgeMessageTests.cs asserts round-trip fidelity), provider response models. Properties are positional or init-only.
P0090: Class: entities with identity and lifecycle (Book, Shelf, Annotation) and services holding behaviour or injected dependencies.
P0091: No mutating behaviour on a record; no settable properties on a record.
P0092: 2.77. Async, Cancellation, and Background Work
P0093: All I/O (SQLite, file system, PDF rendering, HTTP) is async/await; never block with .Result or .Wait().
P0094: Every public async method accepts and forwards a CancellationToken as its last parameter. Workers honour cancellation within 1 second; the UI thread must never stall longer than 100 ms (NFR-PROD-005, verified by UI-layer tests).
P0095: ConfigureAwait(false) in Application, Infrastructure, Workers, and Reader library code; the Avalonia layer may omit it where continuation must resume on the UI thread.
P0096: async void only for Avalonia event handlers, and only with a try/catch that routes failure to the error channel.
P0097: 2.88. Error Handling and Per-File Scan Isolation
P0098: A failure on one PDF never aborts the batch: each file is processed in its own try/catch and the failure is recorded against that BookFile with file path, operation, and exception type. Verified by tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests/Ingestion/ScanHealthTests.cs and IngestionPipelineTests.cs.
P0099: Catch the narrowest exception type; catch (Exception) only at a job boundary that records and continues.
P0100: Never swallow an exception silently: rethrow, record structurally, or return a result type the caller inspects.
P0101: Background jobs are idempotent and resumable — re-running after abnormal termination duplicates no completed work (NFR-OGMA-009; tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests/Ingestion/JobManagementTests.cs, tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests/Ocr/OcrJobQueueServiceTests.cs).
P0102: Deliberate stubs are permitted only for explicitly deferred requirements and must throw, not silently no-op: the V2 answer mode in AdvisorService throws NotImplementedException by design (FR-AI-008 is V2 scope). Do not add new stubs without a requirement ID and phase reference in the XML doc comment.
P0103: 2.99. Golden Corpus Usage
P0104: The golden-corpus harness lives in tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests/GoldenCorpus/:
P0105: SyntheticCorpusGenerator.cs builds seed-pinned synthetic fixtures (QuestPDF-generated), so no copyrighted PDFs live in the repo and every run is reproducible.
P0106: ManifestVerifier.cs checks fixture hashes against the manifest — an unintended fixture change fails the build.
P0107: GoldenCorpusTests.cs and tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests/Ocr/OcrGoldenCorpusTests.cs are the entry points; tests/golden-corpus/ocr-pipeline/ holds OCR pipeline fixtures.
P0108: Rules: any change to scan, render, extract, enrich, or OCR logic must keep the golden-corpus tests green; a new fixture ships in the same PR as its manifest entry and its expected-handling oracle.
P0109: 2.1010. Spikes Are Excluded From Production Standards
P0110: spikes/ holds throwaway proofs (S1–S7, results in spikes/RESULTS.md). spikes/Directory.Build.props shields them from the solution-wide properties. Spike code never moves to src/ by copy-paste; it is re-implemented under full standards with tests. Spike measurements are dev-box trend data, never acceptance evidence (see docs/governance/REFERENCE-HARDWARE.md).
P0111: 2.1111. Commits, Branches, and Traceability
P0112: Binding definitions live in the repo governance docs and are summarized in CONTRIBUTING.md (this phase):
P0113: Conventional Commits per docs/governance/CONVENTIONAL-COMMITS.md, enforced locally by the .github/hooks/commit-msg hook and re-checked in CI. DCO sign-off (git commit -s) required.
P0114: Branch model per docs/governance/BRANCH-STRATEGY.md: main (signed releases only) ← release/*/hotfix/*; develop is the integration branch; feature/<ID>-<slug> per story.
P0115: Every PR lists the FR/NFR/CTRL identifiers it implements; a change to a baselined requirement needs a CIA entry with rollback plan (docs/governance/CIA-WORKFLOW.md).
P0116: 2.1212. Test Naming and Structure
P0117: Unit/integration tests live in tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests organized by module folder (Ai, Catalogue, ClassroomClient, GoldenCorpus, Ingestion, LanHost, Metadata, Ocr, Pdf, Privacy, Reader, SchoolAdmin, Search, Security, Shelf3D, App). Headless UI tests live in tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests.Ui (Avalonia.Headless.XUnit); architecture tests in tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests.Architecture.
P0118: Name pattern MethodUnderTest_Scenario_ExpectedOutcome; Arrange-Act-Assert with blank-line separation; one behaviour per test.
P0119: No production project contains test code; no test asserts true — every test carries a deterministic oracle (see 05-testing-documentation/29119-deterministic-checks.md).
P0120: 2.1313. Code Review Checklist
P0121: Build passes with warnings-as-errors on Windows and macOS (dotnet build OgmaLibrary.sln).
P0122: dotnet format --verify-no-changes reports a clean tree.
P0123: Architecture tests green (dotnet test tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests.Architecture).
P0124: New public members in library projects carry XML docs (CS1591 would fail the build anyway — confirm the docs say something, not /// <summary>Gets or sets.</summary>).
P0125: New user-facing strings externalized in en + fr .resx; pseudolocale tests pass (OGMA0001 analyzer not yet available — reviewer checks by hand).
P0126: Per-file failure isolation preserved on any scan/render/extract path.
P0127: Golden-corpus tests green; new fixtures carry manifest entry + oracle.
P0128: PR lists requirement IDs; CIA entry attached if a baselined FR/NFR/CTRL changed.
P0129: No secret, no inbound listener outside the opt-in LAN host context, no HTTP outside Infrastructure.
P0130: Destructive operations are reversible (backup → diff → verify → restore) per NFR-PROD-010.
P0131: \newpage

P0184: 3.33. Verification
P0185: 3.3.13.1 Test suite
P0186: dotnet test OgmaLibrary.sln --nologo
P0187: Expected estate: 626 [Fact]/[Theory] declarations across 120 declaring files in 3 projects — OgmaLibrary.Tests (unit/integration including the golden corpus), OgmaLibrary.Tests.Ui (Avalonia headless), and OgmaLibrary.Tests.Architecture (NetArchTest). Theory expansion produced 800 executed cases in the 2026-08-13 Release run. See 05-testing-documentation/test-completion-report.md.
P0188: 3.3.23.2 Vulnerability-audit gate
P0189: The July 2026 NU1903 restore blocker for SQLitePCLRaw.lib.e_sqlite3 2.1.10 is closed. The inspected baseline uses EF Core SQLite 10.0.9 and SQLitePCLRaw.bundle_e_sqlite3 3.0.3. The canonical Release test and package-vulnerability audit were green on 2026-08-13. Keep NuGet audit enabled:
P0190: dotnet list OgmaLibrary.sln package --vulnerable --include-transitive
P0191: Do not suppress NU1903 or disable NuGetAudit in CI. A new finding blocks the affected baseline until the vulnerable dependency is removed or a time-bounded, owner-approved waiver is recorded.
P0192: 3.3.33.3 Application launch
P0193: dotnet run --project src/OgmaLibrary.App
P0194: The Avalonia shell window opens. Cold-start timing on a developer machine is trend-only; the 3-second NFR-OGMA-001 budget binds on W-REF-01 at Phase 20 (docs/governance/REFERENCE-HARDWARE.md).
P0195: Point the app at a folder of sample PDFs and start a scan; the catalogue lists the books and thumbnail generation runs in the background.
P0196: A deliberately malformed PDF is recorded as a per-file failure in the scan health report while the batch completes (per-file isolation, FR-LIB-005/FR-LIB-007).
P0197: Open the 3D shelf; a rendered Three.js scene confirms the WebView2 runtime. A blank panel means the runtime is missing.
P0198: 3.3.43.4 Architecture gate
P0199: dotnet test tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests.Architecture --nologo
P0200: All layering tests green — this is the fastest signal that your environment restored the right project graph.
P0201: 3.44. Reference Hardware Is Not Your Laptop
P0202: Performance numbers you measure locally are trend-only. Formal NFR acceptance runs on W-REF-01 (i5-10210U, 8 GB, SATA SSD, UHD 620, Windows 10 22H2) and M-REF-01 (MacBook Air M1, 8 GB, macOS 13.6) at Phase 20 per docs/governance/REFERENCE-HARDWARE.md. Never record a local benchmark as gate evidence.

P0204: 4Contributing to Ogma Library
P0205: This guide defines how work enters the Ogma Library codebase. It mirrors — and where it differs, defers to — the binding governance documents in the repository: docs/governance/BRANCH-STRATEGY.md, docs/governance/CONVENTIONAL-COMMITS.md, docs/governance/CIA-WORKFLOW.md, and docs/governance/HYBRID-GATE.md, plus the repo-root CONTRIBUTING.md and DCO.md. A change that does not follow this guide is not ready to merge.
P0206: The product is delivered under a Hybrid (Water-Scrum-Fall) methodology: the Phase 02 SRS is a signed baseline, and Agile build phases deliver against it. Every code change traces back to a baselined requirement.
P0207: 4.11. Branch Strategy (trunk-with-integration)
P0208: Per docs/governance/BRANCH-STRATEGY.md:
P0209: Branch
P0210: Purpose
P0211: Rules
P0212: main
P0213: Signed releases only
P0214: No direct commits; only merges from release/* and hotfix/* via signed merge commits; fast-forward merges forbidden; every commit on main corresponds to a tagged, signed build
P0215: develop
P0216: Integration of completed work
P0217: PRs merge here after review; each merged commit builds and passes tests on its own
P0218: feature/<ID>-<slug>
P0219: One story or defect
P0220: Branch from develop; example feature/LIB-003-content-hash-identity; rebase on develop before opening a PR; delete after merge
P0221: fix/<ID>-<slug>
P0222: Defect fix
P0223: Same rules as feature branches
P0224: release/<semver>
P0225: Stabilization
P0226: Branch from develop at scope freeze; only release-blocking fixes; merges to main (tag + sign) and back to develop
P0227: hotfix/<semver>
P0228: Urgent fix to a released version
P0229: Branch from main; merges to main and develop
P0230: develop builds feed the Dev release channel; promotion Dev → Alpha → Beta → Stable re-points the already-signed artifact and never rebuilds (ADR-0009; see the Phase 06 deployment guide).
P0231: 4.22. Conventional Commits and DCO
P0232: Per docs/governance/CONVENTIONAL-COMMITS.md, enforced locally by .github/hooks/commit-msg (install via .github/hooks/install-hooks.sh or .ps1) and re-checked in CI:
P0233: Format: type(scope)!: subject — type one of feat, fix, docs, test, refactor, chore, perf, ci, build, style, revert; scope is the bounded context or project (domain, application, infrastructure, reader, workers, bookshelf3d, search, ai, app, host, build, ci); ! plus a BREAKING CHANGE: footer marks a breaking change.
P0234: Subject: imperative, ≤ 72 characters, no trailing period.
P0235: Footers: reference the requirement or story (Implements FR-LIB-005, Closes READ-004) and the mandatory DCO sign-off Signed-off-by: Name <email> (git commit -s).
P0236: Example from the governance doc:
P0237: feat(workers): isolate per-file scan failuresEach file is processed in its own try/catch; a parse failure is recordedagainst the BookFile and the batch continues.Implements FR-LIB-005Signed-off-by: Jane Developer <jane@example.com>
P0238: 4.33. Traceability and Change Impact Analysis
P0239: Every PR lists the FR/NFR/CTRL identifiers it implements. ID families: FR-LIB, FR-CAT, FR-META, FR-READ, FR-SEARCH, FR-AI, FR-LAN, FR-CLIENT, FR-ADMIN, FR-UX, FR-EXT, NFR-OGMA, NFR-PROD, CTRL-OGMA.
P0240: Routine work implementing an already-baselined requirement needs only the standard PR checklist. A change that alters a baselined FR/NFR/CTRL, changes a ratified ADR, adds/removes a bounded-context dependency edge, or changes a cross-context public contract (IAiProvider, ICatalogueReadModel) requires a full CIA entry per docs/governance/CIA-WORKFLOW.md, including a rollback plan and owner sign-off by Peter Bamuhigire.
P0241: The CIA checklist in every PR covers: bounded contexts affected; requirement IDs touched; baseline alteration; i18n (en + fr externalization, pseudolocale pass); icons and accessibility; reversibility of destructive operations; privacy/egress through the AI gateway with payload preview; cross-platform green on Windows and macOS.
P0242: 4.44. Definition of Ready / Definition of Done
P0243: Authoritative DoR/DoD checklists live with the Phase 07 agile artifacts. Minimum bar:
P0244: Ready: traces to a baselined requirement; acceptance criteria have deterministic pass/fail oracles; names its privacy tier where AI or off-device behaviour is involved.
P0245: Done: merged via reviewed PR; new and existing tests pass on Windows and macOS (CI matrix in .github/workflows/ci.yml requires both); golden-corpus suite green; coding standards hold; hybrid validation gate passes.
P0246: 4.55. Code Review Expectations
P0247: At least one approving review; no self-approval.
P0248: The reviewer confirms: layering intact (architecture tests are the backstop, but a reviewer catches intent); per-file scan isolation preserved; requirement IDs listed and criteria verifiable; user strings externalized (the OGMA0001 analyzer does not exist yet — this is a manual check plus pseudolocale tests); PR small enough to review in one sitting.
P0249: 4.66. Security Expectations
P0250: No secret in the repository — keys, tokens, passwords, credentialed connection strings. Provider credentials live in the OS credential store (tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests/Security/PasswordProviderTests.cs covers the credential flow).
P0251: No inbound listener outside the opt-in LAN host context. ArchTests_StandaloneMode_HasNoOpenListener enforces that standalone mode opens nothing; FR-LAN-001 requires host mode to be explicit, reversible, and audited.
P0252: Every off-device call routes through the AI gateway chokepoint (Architecture_OnlyInfrastructureUsesHttpClient, Architecture_AdvisorService_UsesOnlyAiGateway) and respects the active privacy tier and payload preview (FR-ADMIN-007, NFR-PROD-011).
P0253: A change touching personal data or off-device transmission is flagged for the Phase 09 privacy review; classroom features involving minors trigger the DPIA screening path (SchoolDpiaScreeningServiceTests.cs covers the implemented screen).
P0254: 4.77. Pre-PR Gate
P0255: Run all four before requesting review; a failure in any means the PR is not ready:
P0256: python -m engine validate Ogma-Library        # hybrid gate, from the srs-skills workspacedotnet format OgmaLibrary.sln --verify-no-changesdotnet build OgmaLibrary.sln --configuration Debugdotnet test OgmaLibrary.sln --nologo
P0257: The July 2026 NU1903 finding is resolved in the current baseline. Do not merge a change that suppresses NuGet audit; dotnet list OgmaLibrary.sln package --vulnerable --include-transitive must remain green.

P0001: Data Protection Impact Assessment
P0002: Governance and Compliance — Personal Product and Classroom / School Deployments
P0003: 2026-07-06
P0004: Table of Contents
P0006: 1Data Protection Impact Assessment — Ogma Library
P0009: Document
P0010: Data Protection Impact Assessment (DPIA)
P0011: Project
P0012: Ogma Library
P0013: Owner
P0014: Chwezi Core Systems
P0015: Author
P0016: Peter Bamuhigire, Lead Consultant, Chwezi Core
P0017: Version
P0018: 2.0
P0019: Date
P0020: 2026-07-06
P0021: Status
P0022: Draft for owner sign-off
P0023: Classification
P0024: Confidential — Internal Use Only
P0025: Version 1.0 (2026-05-30) was a screening-level assessment of the single-user product, whose baseline posture — the user's own data on the user's own device — is low risk. Version 2.0 expands the assessment because the implemented classroom product (grand-plan Phases 16–18; FR-LAN, FR-CLIENT, FR-ADMIN) processes students' — typically minors' — reading behaviour and AI-query data on behalf of schools. That changes the roles (a school becomes a data controller; the host operator processes on its behalf), the data subjects (children, owed heightened protection under every modern data-protection regime), and the risk profile (institutional surveillance potential, third-party AI exposure).
P0026: Jurisdiction framing: Ogma is jurisdiction-configurable. This assessment uses a GDPR-aligned baseline (the strictest widely applicable standard, and the one the productivity domain profile names) while the deploying school's local Data Protection Act governs each actual deployment. The owner decision on target jurisdictions for classroom sales is open (audit finding F-14) and is required before any classroom pilot.
P0027: This document is product-design compliance guidance, not legal advice, and replaces no jurisdiction-specific legal review.
P0028: \newpage
P0029: 21. Processing Overview
P0030: 2.11.1 Two Deployment Postures, Two Risk Profiles
P0031: Personal posture (single user). Ogma manages the user's own PDF collection on the user's own machine. The user is the data subject for their own content; Ogma holds no regulated third-party PII baseline, transmits nothing by default (privacy tier Offline, per the AI gateway decision ADR-0007), and creates no controller/processor relationship for fully local or local-model operation. The v1.0 analysis of this posture stands and is retained in §§1.3–1.4.
P0032: Classroom posture (implemented, Phases 16–18). A school runs a host that serves a curated collection to enrolled clients; students read, annotate, search, and perform AI-assisted smart searches. Here the school is the data controller for student personal data; Ogma is software the school operates (the product owner is not a processor unless a future hosted service changes that — see the trigger register, §6). The data subjects include minors, which raises the required protection level: defaults must protect without relying on the child's configuration choices.
P0033: 2.21.2 Classroom Processing Inventory
P0034: #
P0035: Processing operation
P0036: Data categories
P0037: Data subjects
P0038: Where processed/stored
P0039: Purpose
P0040: Lawful basis (GDPR-aligned baseline)
P0041: P-1
P0042: Profile enrollment and authentication (FR-CLIENT-002, FR-CLIENT-003, FR-ADMIN-003)
P0043: Name/alias, role, credential hash, enrollment token
P0044: Students, teachers
P0045: Host catalogue (school premises)
P0046: Provide per-student access with private state
P0047: School's public-task/legitimate-interest basis for providing education, per local law; not consent (power imbalance)
P0048: P-2
P0049: Per-student reading state (FR-CLIENT-002, FR-CLIENT-006)

P0112: 2.31.3 Personal-Posture Data Categories (retained from v1.0)
P0113: Category
P0114: Description
P0115: Off-device exposure
P0116: User-Owned Content
P0117: PDFs, extracted text, annotations, notes
P0118: Only under content-aware cloud AI (Tier 2), minimised and previewed
P0119: Catalogue Metadata
P0120: Title, author, tags, progress, statistics
P0121: Only under metadata-only cloud AI (Tier 1) or opt-in metadata lookups, minimised and previewed
P0122: Provider Secrets
P0123: API keys and tokens
P0124: Never as payload; OS credential storage (CTRL-001)
P0125: AI History and Embeddings
P0126: Stored queries, cached responses, vectors
P0127: Local (sidecar per the storage decision ADR-0005); erasable (CTRL-023)
P0128: 2.41.4 Off-Device Flow Summary (both postures)
P0129: Default state: nothing is transmitted (Tier 0 Offline; host mode off by default per FR-LAN-001).
P0130: Tier 1 metadata-only cloud and metadata-provider lookups: minimised, previewed, consented metadata subsets.
P0131: Tier 2 content-aware cloud: minimised, previewed content excerpts; per-library or per-query opt-in; in classrooms additionally gated on the administrator (FR-ADMIN-006).
P0132: Tier 3 local model: on-device inference; nothing leaves the machine.
P0133: Classroom LAN traffic (catalogue, streams, sync): TLS on the local network only (FR-LAN-002); not "off-device" in the cloud sense but treated as a trust boundary in §3.
P0134: \newpage
P0135: 32. Necessity and Proportionality
P0136: 3.12.1 Lawful Basis — Personal Posture
P0137: Fully local and local-model operation (Tiers 0 and 3) creates no controller or processor relationship: the user processes their own data on their own device. For any off-device transmission (Tiers 1–2, metadata lookups) the lawful basis is consent — explicit, specific, informed, per tier and per provider, never bundled into general acceptance (CTRL-019). The default is no transmission.
P0138: 3.22.2 Lawful Basis — Classroom Posture (students, typically minors)
P0139: Consent is not the appropriate basis for core classroom processing: a student cannot freely refuse their school. The GDPR-aligned baseline therefore anchors P-1 through P-4 and P-6 through P-10 (see §1.2) on the school's education-provision basis under its local law (public task or legitimate interest, as the local Data Protection Act frames it), with the school as controller. Consequences the design already honours:
P0140: Protection cannot depend on the child's choices: the class privacy tier defaults to metadata-only (FR-ADMIN-006) and content-aware analysis is gated on the administrator, not the student.
P0141: Transparency is owed to the student regardless of basis: the tier label and payload preview are shown to the student before any off-device call (FR-ADMIN-007), and teacher per-student visibility is disclosed to the students it covers (FR-CLIENT-012).
P0142: Where a deployment's local law requires parental/guardian consent for third-party AI processing of a minor's data (P-5), obtaining it is the school's controller obligation; Ogma supports it by making the payload scope demonstrable (preview + audit log) so the school can describe exactly what is shared.
P0143: 3.32.3 Purpose Limitation
P0144: Each off-device call carries a stated purpose (lookup, recommendation, content-aware analysis) and the consent or policy record is scoped to it; re-use for a new purpose requires a fresh decision. Classroom accounting data (P-7) exists for cost control and is minimised to counts and costs — it is not a pedagogical surveillance feed and is not joined to reading-state detail.
P0145: 3.42.4 Data Minimisation
P0146: The payload for any off-device call is the minimum the purpose requires (CTRL-020), shown exactly in the payload preview before send (CTRL-017). In classrooms the minimisation is structural: the default payload class excludes document content entirely; the host proxies every call so the provider sees the school's account, not per-student provider accounts; and sync blobs are opaque to the host (encrypted client-side, P-3).
P0147: 3.52.5 Local-First and LAN-Only Defaults
P0148: The proportionality control of first resort is architecture. Personal: Tier 0 default, everything useful offline. Classroom: host mode off by default (FR-LAN-001), serving only admin-published content (FR-LAN-009), on the local network only (FR-LAN-002); student reading state stays on premises. The common case for both postures exposes zero data to any external party.
P0149: 3.62.6 No Training on Anyone's Data
P0150: The provider no-training flag defaults to opt-out; enabling training requires a separate explicit opt-in (CTRL-022). For classrooms this decision belongs to the school administrator and is never reachable by a student.
P0151: 3.72.7 Jurisdiction Configuration and Cross-Border Awareness
P0152: Where a chosen provider processes data outside the deployment's jurisdiction, the processing region is surfaced before first use (CTRL-021) and shown in the payload preview. The product ships a GDPR-aligned baseline; each school deployment inherits its local Data Protection Act, and the compliance annex for a given market is produced at sale time.
P0153: [CONTEXT-GAP: target classroom jurisdictions are an open owner decision (audit finding F-14, due 2026-08-21). Until decided, the lawful-basis mapping in §2.2 and the cross-border-transfer analysis for P-5 remain at baseline depth and no classroom pilot may ship.]
P0154: \newpage
P0155: 43. Risk Assessment
P0156: Likelihood and impact are rated Low, Medium, or High after the stated mitigation. Personal-posture risks (PR-) are retained from v1.0; classroom and minors-specific risks (M-) are new in v2.0. Cross-references: security risks live in ../risk-register.md (notably RISK-018, RISK-019); controls in the SRS §7 (CTRL-001 through CTRL-024).

P0189: ID
P0190: Risk
P0191: L
P0192: I
P0193: Mitigation

P0195: Teacher dashboards become covert surveillance of minors' reading behaviour
P0196: Medium
P0197: High
P0198: Aggregate-only dashboard design with per-student visibility disclosed to the students it covers (FR-CLIENT-012); accounting data minimised to counts (P-7) and not joined to reading detail; audit finding review at each classroom-feature change
P0199: M-2
P0200: A student's book content or identifying query text reaches a third-party AI provider
P0201: Low
P0202: High
P0203: Metadata-only class default with content-aware gated on the administrator (FR-ADMIN-006); student-visible tier label and payload preview before every send (FR-ADMIN-007); host proxy hides student identity behind the school account (FR-ADMIN-005); no-training default (CTRL-022)
P0204: M-3
P0205: Student AI query history accumulates into a behavioural profile
P0206: Medium
P0207: Medium
P0208: Local per-profile history; student self-deletion (FR-ADMIN-012); institution-wide purge (FR-ADMIN-013); quota data minimised (P-7)
P0209: M-4
P0210: Peer exposure: one student sees another's reading state, notes, or AI history on shared machines
P0211: Low
P0212: High
P0213: Per-profile private databases and role scoping (FR-CLIENT-002, FR-CLIENT-003, FR-CLIENT-008); profile isolation tests in the codebase
P0214: M-5
P0215: Host operator (school IT) reads student sync data
P0216: Low
P0217: Medium
P0218: Sync blobs are encrypted client-side and opaque to the host (FR-CLIENT-006 implementation); host audit records access attempts (CTRL-018)
P0219: M-6
P0220: LAN adversary intercepts or spoofs the host (student data in transit; fake host harvesting credentials)
P0221: Medium
P0222: High
P0223: TLS with certificate provisioning and trust-on-first-use fingerprint verification (FR-LAN-004); fingerprint re-approval on change; adversarial verification scheduled Phase 19 (RISK-018)
P0224: M-7
P0225: Revocation or device loss strands or exposes a student's local notes
P0226: Low
P0227: Medium
P0228: Revocation removes host access but preserves local notes (FR-CLIENT-009); at-rest encryption option arrives with Phase 19 (trigger DT-1, §6) — until then local notes rely on OS account security, a stated limitation
P0229: M-8
P0230: Quota/audit records themselves over-collect (per-student logs become a secondary surveillance channel)
P0231: Medium
P0232: Medium
P0233: Audit captures security events and counts, not reading content; export is admin-scoped and audited; retention governed by the school's controller policy, with purge available (FR-ADMIN-013)
P0234: 4.33.3 Residual Risk Statement
P0235: Personal posture: residual risk is Low for local and local-model operation (nothing leaves the device) and Low-to-Medium for opt-in cloud tiers, held there by default-deny, the payload-preview consent gate, the single egress chokepoint, scoped erasure, and per-transmission audit. The dominant variable is the user's own confirmed choice, made visible and reversible.
P0236: Classroom posture: residual risk is Medium overall and concentrated in M-1, M-2, and M-6. The design mitigations are implemented in code (Phases 16–18, with DPIA screening built into the school AI flow — "feat: screen school ai dpia"); what remains is operational and jurisdictional: Phase 19 adversarial verification of the LAN surface, the at-rest encryption option (DT-1), and the school-side controller obligations (transparency notices, any parental-consent requirement) that Ogma can support but not perform. Classroom residual risk cannot be finally accepted until the target-jurisdiction decision (§2.7) closes.
P0237: No processing in either posture meets the GDPR-aligned threshold of "high residual risk after mitigation" that would mandate prior consultation with a supervisory authority — provided the required actions in §5 are completed before the affected features ship.
P0238: \newpage
P0239: 54. Data Subject Rights
P0240: Rights are implemented as user-operable controls, not policy statements. Section 4.1 covers the personal posture (the user is their own data subject); §4.2 covers students and teachers in classroom deployments, where the school as controller answers rights requests and Ogma provides the mechanisms.
P0241: 5.14.1 Personal Posture
P0242: Erasure: delete AI query history and derived embeddings per document or whole corpus in one operation, including cached provider responses; the provider's own deletion path is surfaced for anything sent off-device (CTRL-023).
P0243: Portability: export catalogue, annotations, and metadata in documented open formats via the portable bundle of catalogue database plus sidecar (ADR-0005, NFR-PROD-009); exports never contain provider secrets (CTRL-015).
P0244: Withdrawal: return to Tier 0 at any time; removing a provider deletes its stored key (CTRL-003). Withdrawal stops future transmission immediately and pairs with erasure for past data.
P0245: Inspection: the payload preview before every send (CTRL-017, CTRL-020) and the exportable local audit log of every sensitive action (CTRL-018) make each disclosure visible and refusable in advance and reviewable afterwards.
P0246: 5.24.2 Classroom Posture — Students and Teachers
P0247: Right (GDPR-aligned baseline)
P0248: Mechanism in Ogma
P0249: Who executes
P0250: Access — what is held about me
P0251: Per-profile reading state and AI history are visible in the student's own client; host audit export shows a profile's security events
P0252: Student directly; school for audit extracts
P0253: Erasure — my AI history
P0254: Student self-deletion of own AI query history (FR-ADMIN-012)
P0255: Student directly
P0256: Erasure — institutional
P0257: Purge of all student AI history for the institution (FR-ADMIN-013); profile deletion on revocation flow (FR-ADMIN-003)
P0258: School administrator
P0259: Objection to monitoring
P0260: Teacher visibility is aggregate-only with per-student views disclosed (FR-CLIENT-012); the disclosure gives the student/guardian the knowledge needed to object to the school
P0261: School (controller decision), informed by the disclosed design
P0262: Portability
P0263: A student's local notes remain theirs after revocation (FR-CLIENT-009); local notes export with the standard export tooling
P0264: Student directly
P0265: Transparency
P0266: Tier label and exact payload preview before any off-device AI call (FR-ADMIN-007); host certificate fingerprint shown at enrollment (FR-LAN-004)
P0267: Built into the flows
P0268: Rectification
P0269: Profile edits (FR-ADMIN-003); metadata corrections follow the standard provenance-tracked edit path
P0270: School administrator; student for own notes
P0271: Minors' rights are typically exercised by or with parents/guardians under the deployment's local law; the school's controller process routes those requests, and every mechanism above is operable by the administrator on a guardian's behalf. Ogma's contribution is that no right requires vendor intervention: erasure, access, and portability are self-service at the school.
P0272: \newpage
P0273: 65. Conclusion and Required Actions
P0274: 6.15.1 Assessment Outcome
P0275: Personal posture: Low baseline risk, unchanged from v1.0. The product processes the user's own data on the user's own device, defaults to Tier 0 Offline, and exposes data only at one opt-in boundary governed by default-deny, a single egress chokepoint, payload-preview consent, scoped erasure, and per-transmission audit.
P0276: Classroom posture: Medium residual risk, conditionally acceptable. The implemented design (Phases 16–18) already embodies the protective defaults a minors-processing product needs — metadata-only class default, student-visible payload previews, aggregate-only disclosed dashboards, per-profile isolation, opaque sync, LAN-only TLS transport, deletion and purge controls. Acceptance is conditional on the required actions below.
P0277: 6.25.2 Required Actions Before Shipping Off-Device Features (personal posture, carried from v1.0 — all implemented, verification standing)
P0278: Payload preview before every send, with recorded confirmation (CTRL-017, CTRL-020) — implemented; re-verify at each release gate.
P0279: Consent capture per tier and per provider, never bundled (CTRL-019) — implemented; re-verify at each release gate.
P0280: Erasure of AI history and embeddings by document or corpus, with provider deletion path surfaced (CTRL-023) — implemented; re-verify at each release gate.
P0281: User-viewable, exportable audit of every off-device transmission, tier change, and credential change (CTRL-018) — implemented; re-verify at each release gate.
P0282: 6.35.3 Required Actions Before Any Classroom Pilot or Sale
P0283: Jurisdiction decision (owner): name the target classroom jurisdictions so §2.2's lawful-basis mapping and §2.7's transfer analysis can be finalised per market — due 2026-08-21 (audit finding F-14). Blocking.
P0284: Phase 19 adversarial verification of the LAN surface (TLS/TOFU, enrollment, streaming, path validation) per risk RISK-018 — Sprint-1, due 2026-07-24. Blocking.
P0285: School-controller pack: a deployment guide stating the school's controller obligations (transparency notice templates for students/guardians, retention policy hooks, parental-consent guidance where local law requires it for P-5) — with the Phase 22 packaging work. Blocking for sale, not for internal pilot.
P0286: At-rest encryption option (trigger DT-1, §6) before classroom deployments on shared or loanable hardware — Phase 19, US-052.
P0287: 6.45.4 Review Triggers
P0288: This DPIA is revisited before any off-device feature's release gate, whenever a new provider or privacy tier is introduced, on any classroom-pilot incident involving student data, and at every trigger in §6.
P0289: 6.55.5 Status of This Document
P0290: Product-design compliance guidance, not legal advice; it replaces no jurisdiction-specific legal review. Version 2.0, dated 2026-07-06, draft for owner sign-off; supersedes the v0.1 screening of 2026-05-30.
P0291: \newpage
P0292: 76. DPIA Trigger Register
P0293: Planned features that will alter the data-protection posture are registered here before they are built. Each trigger names the change, the assessment it requires, and its gate: the feature's story cannot pass the Definition of Done until the trigger's assessment is complete and signed off. This register operationalises the domain rule that a DPIA screening precedes any feature sending user content off-device, and extends it to custody-changing features.
P0294: ID
P0295: Planned feature (phase)
P0296: Posture change
P0297: Assessment required
P0298: Gate
P0299: DT-1
P0300: At-rest encryption of catalogue and sidecar (Phase 19, story US-052)
P0301: Adds key custody: a lost key means lost annotations; a weak default means false assurance. Affects students' local notes on shared hardware (risk M-7)
P0302: Update §1 inventory (key material handling), §3 (key-loss and recovery risks), and the school-controller pack (key policy for shared machines); confirm no key material ever reaches logs or exports (CTRL-002 extension)
P0303: US-052 Definition of Done
P0304: DT-2
P0305: Encrypted cloud sync, V2 (out of MVP; data model designed to allow it)
P0306: First feature moving reading state off premises: introduces a processor relationship (sync operator), cross-border storage questions, and a new breach surface for minors' data if offered to schools
P0307: Full DPIA revision before build: controller/processor role mapping, transfer mechanism per target jurisdiction (depends on the F-14 decision), breach-notification obligations, retention/erasure semantics for server-side blobs
P0308: V2 planning gate; no sync code merges before the revised DPIA is signed
P0309: DT-3
P0310: Extension SDK and local read API (Phase 23, stories US-058, US-059)
P0311: Third-party plugins gain read access to catalogue and index; a malicious plugin is a new exfiltration path
P0312: Verify that all plugin off-device traffic is architecturally forced through the gateway tiers (FR-EXT-001) with the payload preview intact; add plugin-conduct terms to the SDK docs
P0313: US-058 Definition of Done
P0314: DT-4
P0315: Classroom answer mode surfaces (depends on FR-AI-008 / FR-ADMIN-011 completion, Sprint-5)

P0318: US-028 Definition of Done
P0319: DT-5

P0321: First product-analytics data flow; device-local by default per the domain profile
P0322: Confirm telemetry is opt-in, device-local by default, content-free, and separately consented; document its inventory row before the beta channel opens
P0323: US-057 Definition of Done
P0324: Adding a feature that transmits, stores, or grants access to personal data without a row in this register is itself an audit finding (verify-before-emit rule); the register is reviewed at every sprint planning.

P0030: This document supersedes HLD v0.1 (2026-05-30) under ADR-0015. It describes the architecture in two registers, marked throughout:
P0031: As-built — verified against the codebase at C:\wamp64\www\Ogma-Library (grand-plan Phases 00–18: 7 src projects, 3 test projects, 800 executed cases, 11 EF Core migrations through Phase18SchoolAdminTables), per _context/build-status.md.
P0032: Design-to-be — Phases 19–23: security hardening, formal performance acceptance on reference hardware, full i18n (es/it/de) and the WCAG 2.2 AA audit, packaging/signing/store submission, and launch operations.
P0033: Where a section states a mechanism without a marker, it is as-built. All performance figures before Phase 20 are trend-only; formal NFR acceptance evidence is produced only on W-REF-01/M-REF-01 per _context/reference-hardware.md.
P0035: Confidential — Internal Use Only. This document is the intellectual property of Chwezi Core Systems. It records the high-level architecture of Ogma Library and is not for external distribution. No part may be reproduced or disclosed without written permission.
P0037: \newpage
P0038: 21. Architecture Overview
P0039: 2.11.1 Architectural Style — As Built
P0040: Ogma Library is a local-first modular monolith: one deployable Avalonia 11.3.17 desktop application on the .NET 10 LTS runtime (ADR-0001, ADR-0002), decomposed internally into bounded contexts rather than distributed services. The style is not aspirational — it is enforced by NetArchTest rules in tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests.Architecture/ArchitectureTests.cs that fail the build on violation (Section 2.4). The governing structural rule: the catalogue of record (SQLite via EF Core 10.0.9) is the single source of truth for book identity, and every other context — Reader, Search, AI Advisor, 3D shelf, and now the LAN host and classroom client — reads identity through contracts and never owns it.
P0041: Since baseline v1.0, one deliberate extension changed the deployment picture without changing the style: the same binary now runs in one of three runtime modes (ADR-0010) —
P0042: Standalone (default): the single-user product; no inbound network listener (constraint CI-2 holds).
P0043: Library Host (opt-in): a school's central machine serves catalogue projections, sidecar assets, page renders or validated file streams, and a managed AI proxy to LAN clients over HTTPS.
P0044: Client/Classroom: a student machine discovers and joins a host, reads streamed content, and keeps per-profile private state.
P0045: The mode is runtime configuration, not a separate product. Section 8 specifies the LAN/classroom architecture in full.
P0046: A modular monolith remains the correct style for four grounded reasons:
P0047: The dominant operations — open library, browse, read, annotate, full-text search — must function with no network present and identical result correctness (NFR-PROD-001). No core path takes a network dependency.
P0048: The personal corpus is single-user and on-device; there is no tenant boundary. The classroom track deliberately reuses the projection contracts of the monolith rather than introducing services: clients are projection consumers exactly as the Reader and 3D shelf are in-process.
P0049: In-process cross-context calls are what make the cold-start budget of 3 s P95 (NFR-OGMA-001) and catalogue-load budget of 2 s P95 at 2,000 books (NFR-OGMA-002) reachable on the W-REF-01 reference laptop.
P0050: Clean module boundaries preserved the extension option, and the classroom track proved it: Phases 16–18 landed as new contexts plus decorators (ClassroomCatalogueReadModel, ClassroomBookFileLocator) without rewriting the core.
P0051: 2.21.2 Quality Drivers Tied to Non-Functional Requirements
P0052: Every driver carries a threshold and an owning mechanism; Section 10 adds the verifying test layer per driver.
P0053: Quality driver
P0054: Architectural consequence (as-built mechanism)
P0055: Governing NFR
P0056: Offline correctness
P0057: No core path depends on a network call; AiDisabledProvider is the registered AI default.
P0058: NFR-PROD-001
P0059: Cold start ≤ 3 s P95
P0060: Lazy module activation; deferred index/AI warm-up; IBenchmarkContext instrumentation from startup.
P0061: NFR-OGMA-001, NFR-PROD-002
P0062: Catalogue load ≤ 2 s P95 (2,000 books)
P0063: Virtualised catalogue rendering; thumbnails as sidecar files, never database blobs.
P0064: NFR-OGMA-002
P0065: Search ≤ 150 ms metadata / ≤ 500 ms full-text P95
P0066: MetadataSearchService over indexed columns; FtsIndexService over the SearchFts5 external-content table.
P0067: NFR-OGMA-003, NFR-OGMA-004
P0068: Page turn ≤ 100 ms P95 (cached)
P0069: PageRenderCache with adjacent-page pre-render and memory-budgeted eviction.
P0070: NFR-OGMA-005
P0071: 3D shelf ≥ 60 FPS (500 books)
P0072: Three.js r181 in WebView2/WKWebView; compact scene model over the typed bridge; npm run perf:budget layout baseline.
P0073: NFR-OGMA-006
P0074: UI never blocked > 100 ms
P0075: 4 hosted workers own all long-running work off the UI thread.
P0076: NFR-PROD-005
P0077: Annotation durability
P0078: AnnotationService persists to the catalogue before reporting saved.
P0079: NFR-OGMA-008
P0080: Job recoverability
P0081: Jobs checkpointed per item; JobRecoveryService.RecoverAsync resets interrupted jobs at startup and writes a JobRecovered audit event.
P0082: NFR-OGMA-009
P0083: Privacy-tier transparency
P0084: AiGateway is the sole egress; AiPrivacyService builds the payload preview; architecture test forbids System.Net.Http outside Infrastructure.
P0085: NFR-PROD-011
P0086: Update and migration safety
P0087: CatalogueMigrator applies idempotent migrations behind snapshots; signed updates are Phase 22 design-to-be.
P0088: NFR-PROD-012
P0089: No lock-in
P0090: Source files stay in the user's folder; catalogue plus sidecar form the export bundle.
P0091: NFR-PROD-009
P0092: 2.31.3 System Context (C4 Level 1)
P0093: In Standalone mode, Ogma Library is one application operating on the user's device: it reads a user-owned folder of PDFs, augments it with a local catalogue and sidecar assets, and reaches a small set of optional, privacy-gated off-device services. In Host mode the boundary gains one deliberate inbound surface — the Kestrel LAN listener — serving classroom clients.
P0094:                         ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐                        │                 OGMA LIBRARY                    │                        │   Avalonia modular monolith on .NET 10 LTS      │  ┌──────────────┐      │                                                 │  │ Reader /     │ uses │  Catalogue of record (SQLite + FTS5, EF Core)   │  │ Librarian    │─────▶│  = single source of truth for book identity    │  │ (desktop)    │◀─────│  Sidecar asset folder (content-hash keyed)      │  └──────────────┘      │                                                 │  ┌──────────────┐reads │  Contexts: Catalogue · Ingestion · Enrichment · │  │ User's PDF   │─────▶│  Reader · Search · AI Advisor · Bookshelf 3D ·  │  │ folder       │      │  Settings/Security · Workers · LAN Host ·       │  └──────────────┘      │  Classroom Client · School Admin                │                        └────────┬──────────────────────────┬─────────────┘                    AI gateway = │ sole off-device egress    │ opt-in Kestrel                    (gated, tier-│ enforced, previewed,      │ HTTPS listener                    audited)     ▼ audited)                  ▼ (Host mode only)   ┌────────────────────┐ ┌──────────────────────┐  ┌─────────────────────────┐   │ Metadata providers │ │ AI providers          │  │ LAN classroom clients   │   │ Google Books /     │ │ Anthropic / OpenAI-   │  │ (students, teachers)    │   │ Open Library       │ │ compat / Ollama local │  │ mDNS _ogma._tcp + TLS   │   └────────────────────┘ └──────────────────────┘  └─────────────────────────┘
P0095: Every off-device connector fires only when the active privacy tier permits it and the user has confirmed the previewed payload (NFR-PROD-011), and each such call writes a local audit entry (NFR-PROD-013). The inbound LAN surface exists only when an administrator activates Host mode (ADR-0010); the default install opens no listener.
P0096: 2.41.4 Governing Architecture Decisions
P0097: The structural choices in this document are recorded under 09-governance-compliance/05-adr/. ADR-0001 through ADR-0013 are Accepted, ratified in implementation, with the owner counter-signature pending the baseline v2.0 gate; ADR-0014 and ADR-0015 are Proposed.
P0098: ADR-0001 .NET 10 LTS runtime. ADR-0002 Avalonia shell. ADR-0003 WebView-hosted Three.js 3D shelf (macOS FPS gate open, Phase 20). ADR-0004 PDFium via PDFtoImage behind an adapter. ADR-0005 SQLite catalogue plus sidecar. ADR-0006 hybrid search. ADR-0007 provider-neutral AI gateway with 4 privacy tiers. ADR-0008 database-first annotations. ADR-0009 Velopack + MSIX distribution (implementation Phase 22).
P0099: ADR-0010 opt-in Library Host mode (scoped amendment of CI-2). ADR-0011 local Tesseract OCR. ADR-0012 classroom identity and per-profile private state. ADR-0013 school-managed AI through the host gateway.
P0100: ADR-0014 EF Core 10.0.9 on net10.0 version alignment (open; due Phase 19 entry). ADR-0015 baseline v2.0 supersession of v1.0.
P0101: 2.51.5 Phase 19–23 Design-to-Be Summary
P0102: The as-built system ends at grand-plan Phase 18. Five deltas remain, each named where it touches a section below:
P0103: Phase 19 — Security hardening: STRIDE threat model execution (host surface first), untrusted-PDF isolation hardening beyond project boundaries, optional at-rest encryption, SAST integration, admin-authentication strengthening.
P0104: Phase 20 — Performance acceptance: formal NFR-OGMA measurement on W-REF-01/M-REF-01, including the open macOS ≥ 60 FPS gate and host concurrency budgets.
P0105: Phase 21 — Full i18n and accessibility: Spanish, Italian, German resources; the WCAG 2.2 AA audit as a release gate.
P0106: Phase 22 — Packaging: Velopack feed, MSIX, notarized DMG, code signing, native-asset bundling (PDFium, Tesseract data, WebView2 runtime), the UDP 5353 firewall rule.
P0107: Phase 23 — Beta and launch operations.

P0109: 32. Solution Structure
P0110: 3.12.1 Project Breakdown — As Built
P0111: OgmaLibrary.sln contains 7 source projects and 3 test projects, all targeting net10.0 with warnings-as-errors and mandatory XML documentation. Dependencies flow inward toward the domain; the Avalonia shell is the sole composition root.
P0112: OgmaLibrary.sln│├── src/│   ├── OgmaLibrary.App             Avalonia shell, views, view models, CompositionRoot.cs,│   │                               Themes/Tokens.axaml, Icons/IconCatalog.cs│   ├── OgmaLibrary.Domain          entities, value objects, invariants (no outward dependency)│   ├── OgmaLibrary.Application     use-case contracts and services: Catalogue, Ingestion,│   │                               Metadata, Reader, Search, Ai, Ocr, Commands, Navigation,│   │                               LanHost, ClassroomClient, SchoolAdmin│   ├── OgmaLibrary.Infrastructure  EF Core SQLite (Persistence, Catalogue), Pdf, Ocr,│   │                               Search, AI, Metadata, Ingestion, Sidecar, Security,│   │                               Localization, LanHost, ClassroomClient, SchoolAdmin│   ├── OgmaLibrary.Reader          Session, Cache, TextLayer, Annotations, Progress│   ├── OgmaLibrary.Bookshelf3D     typed C#↔JS bridge contract + embedded scene assets│   └── OgmaLibrary.Workers         BookIngestionWorker, SearchExtractionWorker,│                                   EmbeddingGenerationWorker, Ocr/OcrWorker,│                                   JobRecoveryService├── src/shelf3d                     TypeScript Three.js r181 scene (bundled, no CDN)└── tests/    ├── OgmaLibrary.Tests            unit + integration + golden corpus + migrations    ├── OgmaLibrary.Tests.Ui         Avalonia headless UI tests    └── OgmaLibrary.Tests.Architecture  NetArchTest structural rules
P0113: Project
P0114: Responsibility
P0115: Must not contain (enforced where noted)
P0116: OgmaLibrary.App
P0117: Views, view models, navigation, DI wiring in CompositionRoot.cs.
P0118: Persistence, provider calls, PDF parsing. Only project allowed to reference Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection (test-enforced).
P0119: OgmaLibrary.Domain
P0120: Entities and invariants.
P0121: Any outward dependency (test-enforced).
P0122: OgmaLibrary.Application
P0123: Use-case contracts (IAiGateway, ICatalogueReadModel, ILibraryHostClient, …) and pure services (AdvisorService, HybridRankingService).
P0124: EF Core, HTTP, concrete SQLite/PDF types (test-enforced).
P0125: OgmaLibrary.Infrastructure
P0126: Every concrete adapter: CatalogueDbContext, PdfiumAdapter, TesseractOcrProvider, AiGateway and providers, KestrelHostModeListener, classroom and school-admin services.
P0127: View models or UI code. Sole holder of System.Net.Http and EF Core (test-enforced).
P0128: OgmaLibrary.Reader
P0129: ReaderSessionService, PageRenderCache, TextLayerService, AnnotationService, ReadingProgressService.
P0130: Infrastructure and Search references (test-enforced).
P0131: OgmaLibrary.Bookshelf3D
P0132: Bridge message contract (BridgeCommand/BridgeEvent), IWebViewBridge, scene assets.
P0133: Catalogue truth; it renders a handed projection.
P0134: OgmaLibrary.Workers
P0135: Hosted background workers and JobRecoveryService.
P0136: App-project references (test-enforced); UI-thread work.
P0137: 3.22.2 Dependency Direction
P0138: OgmaLibrary.Domain depends on nothing. OgmaLibrary.Application depends only on Domain and interfaces it declares. Infrastructure, Reader, Bookshelf3D, and Workers implement those interfaces. OgmaLibrary.App references everything solely to compose it.
P0139:         OgmaLibrary.App  (composition root — the only binder)               │   ┌───────────┼───────────┬───────────────┐   ▼           ▼           ▼               ▼ Reader   Bookshelf3D   Workers     Infrastructure   └───────────┴───────────┴───────────────┘                       │ depend on contracts in                       ▼              OgmaLibrary.Application                       │                       ▼                OgmaLibrary.Domain   (no outward dependency)
P0140: 3.32.3 Composition Root — As Built
P0141: src/OgmaLibrary.App/CompositionRoot.cs (AddOgmaLibrary(IServiceCollection)) is the only place a concrete implementation binds to an interface. Everything of architectural consequence is visible in this one file:
P0142: Data: AddCatalogueContext with the data directory defaulting to Ogma Library Data under OS app-data; CatalogueMigrator applies migrations at startup.
P0143: AI: AddAiGatewayCore registers AiGateway with AiDisabledProvider as default — Tier 0 offline is structural, not configured.
P0144: Platform seams: IWebViewBridge resolves to WKWebViewBridge on macOS, WebView2Bridge otherwise; IPasswordProvider resolves to WindowsPasswordProvider (DPAPI), MacOsKeychainPasswordProvider, or UnsupportedPasswordProvider.
P0145: Workers: AddHostedService for BookIngestionWorker, SearchExtractionWorker, EmbeddingGenerationWorker, OcrWorker; JobRecoveryService registered for startup recovery.
P0146: Classroom decorators: ICatalogueReadModel resolves to ClassroomCatalogueReadModel wrapping the standalone CatalogueReadModel; IBookFileLocator resolves to ClassroomBookFileLocator wrapping BookFileLocator — Standalone behaviour is the pass-through case, so client mode adds no forked code path.
P0147: Scaffold gating: AddLanHostServices, AddClassroomClientServices, and AddSchoolAdminServices register the Phase 16–18 contexts in their inactive state; activation is a runtime admin action, never a registration change.
P0148: Because all wiring is centralised, the single egress chokepoint and the bounded-context contracts are enforceable by inspecting one type — and are additionally machine-enforced (Section 2.4).
P0149: 3.42.4 Architecture Tests — The Enforcement Layer
P0150: tests/OgmaLibrary.Tests.Architecture/ArchitectureTests.cs turns the rules above from convention into build gates. The load-bearing rules:
P0151: Architecture_DomainProject_HasNoOutwardDependencies — Domain isolation.
P0152: Architecture_OnlyInfrastructureUsesHttpClient — the single HTTP-egress chokepoint: no project other than Infrastructure may reference System.Net.Http, so no feature module can open its own provider or network connection.
P0153: Architecture_OnlyAppBindsImplementations — App-only dependency-injection binding.
P0154: Architecture_OnlyInfrastructureUsesEntityFrameworkCore — persistence stays behind the boundary (with the named CatalogueDbContext exception).
P0155: Architecture_WorkersProject_HasNoDependencyOnAppProject, Architecture_Reader_DoesNotDependOnInfrastructure, Architecture_Reader_DoesNotDependOnSearch, Architecture_MetadataProviderHttpClients_OnlyInInfrastructure, Architecture_MetadataEnrichment_DoesNotDependOnAiOrOpenAi — context-boundary rules keeping enrichment, reading, and search separable.
P0156: An engineer extending the system inherits these constraints automatically: a violating reference fails dotnet test, not a code review.
P0157: 3.52.5 Design-to-Be Deltas in This Section
P0158: Phase 19: process-level isolation for PDF/OCR workers (today the boundary is project + resource bounds, not a separate OS process).
P0159: Phase 22: packaging projects (Velopack/MSIX/DMG) join the solution; none exist yet (ADR-0009).
P0160: \newpage
P0161: 43. Bounded Contexts
P0162: 4.13.1 Context Catalogue — As Built
P0163: The v1.0 design named 9 contexts; the classroom track added 3 (LAN Host, Classroom Client, School Administration — ADR-0010, ADR-0012, ADR-0013). Each context owns a coherent model and is explicitly forbidden responsibilities that belong to a neighbour. This owns/must-not-own discipline is what keeps the catalogue the single source of truth and prevents derived state — renders, indexes, embeddings, 3D scenes, client caches — from drifting into authority.
P0164: Bounded context
P0165: Owns (key as-built types)
P0166: Must not own
P0167: Library Catalogue
P0168: Book/file identity, availability, metadata, shelves, smart shelves (CatalogueReadModel, CatalogueWriteService, BookIdentityService, SmartShelfCondition).
P0169: Rendering, AI prompts.
P0170: Ingestion Pipeline
P0171: Scanning, hashing, thumbnails, change detection (IngestionOrchestrator, BookIngestionWorker, ScanProgressService).
P0172: User metadata edits.
P0173: Metadata Enrichment
P0174: ISBN detection, Google Books / Open Library lookup, confidence merge, write-back, health (PdfWriteBackService, LibraryHealthService).
P0175: Rendering, reading progress, AI dependencies (test-enforced).
P0176: Reader
P0177: Rendering, navigation, zoom, bookmarks, annotations, citations, reading memory (ReaderSessionService, PageRenderCache, AnnotationService, CitationService, ReadingMemoryService).
P0178: Scan policy, external lookup, Infrastructure/Search references (test-enforced).
P0179: Search Index
P0180: FTS5 index, extraction, chunks, embeddings, ranking (FtsIndexService, SemanticSearchService, CombinedSearchService, HybridRankingService, IndexManagerService).
P0181: UI state.
P0182: AI Advisor
P0183: Recommendation and reading-plan pipelines, provider routing, privacy enforcement (AdvisorService, AiGateway, AiPrivacyService).
P0184: Direct database writes.
P0185: Bookshelf Presentation
P0186: 3D scene state, bridge contract, ogma:// asset serving (Bookshelf3DViewModel, IWebViewBridge).
P0187: Catalogue truth.
P0188: Settings & Security
P0189: Credential storage, privacy tiers, audit state (IPasswordProvider implementations, PasswordCredentialKey, audit repositories).
P0190: AI logic.
P0191: Workers
P0192: Background execution and crash recovery (JobRecoveryService, 4 hosted workers).
P0193: UI-thread work, App references (test-enforced).
P0194: LAN Host (Phase 16)
P0195: The only inbound listener; sessions, publishing, streaming, mDNS, certificates (KestrelHostModeListener, LibraryHostService, ClientSessionService, LanBookFileResolver, MdnsAdvertiser, LocalCertificateProvisioner).
P0196: Standalone credential store; client private state.
P0197: Classroom Client (Phase 17)
P0198: Modes, profiles, host trust, offline cache, sync (ClassroomConnectionService, HostTrustService, StudentPrivateRepository, DiskOfflineCacheService, ClassroomSyncService).
P0199: Host server internals; writes to the standalone catalogue.
P0200: School Administration (Phase 18)
P0201: Publish policy, enrollment, managed AI keys/policy/quotas/audit (SchoolAdminCatalogueService, SchoolAiKeyProvider, SchoolAiPolicyService, AiProxyEndpointHandler, SchoolDpiaScreeningService).
P0202: Key material exposure (APIs expose status only); client-side logic.
P0203: Packaging & Updates (design-to-be, Phase 22)
P0204: Release channels, signing, installers, update checks (ADR-0009).
P0205: Product behaviour.
P0206: 4.23.2 The Catalogue-as-Source-of-Truth Rule
P0207: The governing invariant, stated once and enforced everywhere:
P0208: The Library Catalogue is the single source of truth for book identity. The Reader, AI Advisor, Search Index, Bookshelf Presentation, and every classroom client read book identity through contracts and never own it.
P0209: Consequences, all realised in code:
P0210: Derived state is rebuildable. The FTS5 index, embeddings, thumbnails, spine textures, the 3D scene, and client offline caches are projections; FtsIndexService.CheckIntegrityAsync plus rebuild, the IndexManagerService, and cache purge controls exist precisely because projections may be discarded (NFR-PROD-009).
P0211: The AI Advisor performs no direct database writes; AdvisorService persists history through application contracts, so audit and invariants are never bypassed.
P0212: A book activated in the 3D shelf, grid, list, directory view, or a classroom client resolves to the same book-detail surface (FR-CAT-001), because all views project the same catalogue records — in classroom mode through ClassroomCatalogueReadModel, a decorator over the same read model.
P0213: The host serves projections of its catalogue over the LAN; clients never receive or mutate identity. The classroom track is therefore an extension of the projection discipline, not an exception to it.
P0214: 4.33.3 Context Map
P0215: Contexts communicate through interfaces declared in OgmaLibrary.Application; no context reaches into another's storage.
P0216:                             ┌────────────────────────┐        scan results        │   Library Catalogue    │  read-model projections   ┌───────────────────────▶│  (identity, metadata,     │         │ reviewed writes               │┌──┴───────────┐  contracts     ▼         ▼                 ┌─────────────┴─────┐│ Ingestion +  │         ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────────┐      │ Bookshelf
