P0239: Off-device, outbound (gated)
P0240: Ollama
P0241: Local AI inference and embeddings
P0242: Local, on-device
P0243: Kestrel LAN listener (KestrelHostModeListener)
P0244: Opt-in host-mode serving of catalogue, assets, streams, and the AI proxy
P0245: Inbound, LAN-only, TLS (host mode only)
P0246: mDNS advertisement (_ogma-library._tcp.local)
P0247: Zero-configuration host discovery on the LAN
P0248: LAN broadcast (host mode only)
P0249: SI-1 Provider gateway. All AI provider integrations are reached through the provider-neutral gateway (AiGateway behind the IAiProvider contract); no module bypasses the gateway. In classroom mode the host gateway is the single egress point for the whole class (FR-ADMIN-005).
P0250: SI-2 Off-device gating. Off-device interfaces (Google Books, Open Library, cloud AI) are invoked only when the active privacy tier permits the call and the user has confirmed the previewed payload (Section 7, CTRL-017).
P0251: SI-3 Message bridge contract. The C#-to-JavaScript bridge exchanges a typed scene model outbound and typed interaction events inbound; every inbound message is validated against the contract before it is acted on.
P0252: SI-4 Host API surface. In host mode the listener serves catalogue projections (/api/v1/catalogue), cover/spine/thumbnail assets, range-request PDF content streams, reading-state sync, and the AI proxy endpoint; every request is authenticated per profile and audited (FR-LAN-002, FR-LAN-006, FR-LAN-010). Only admin-published folders and shelves are reachable (FR-LAN-009).
P0253: 4.43.4 Communications Interfaces
P0254: CI-1. All off-device calls use an authenticated, integrity-checked transport (Transport Layer Security, TLS). Update metadata and artifacts are fetched over an authenticated, integrity-checked channel, and the update descriptor is signature-verified independently of transport (Section 7, CTRL-012 and CTRL-013).
P0255: CI-2. In Standalone and Classroom Client modes the application opens no inbound network listener. The sole exception is the opt-in Library Host mode (FR-LAN-001), which introduces a deliberately designed, TLS-protected, LAN-bounded, authenticated inbound surface with its own threat model and controls (Section 7.8). This scoping of the v1.0 CI-2 rule is recorded in ADR-0010 (docs/plans/grand-plan/LAN-CLASSROOM-ARCHITECTURE.md §1); the default install behaves exactly as v1.0 specified.
P0256: CI-3. Every off-device communication produces a local audit entry naming the provider, purpose, payload scope, and active privacy tier (NFR-PROD-013; CTRL-018). In host mode, every LAN request additionally produces an AuditEvents record naming the client identity and resource (FR-LAN-010).
P0257: CI-4. LAN traffic never travels in plaintext: the host provisions a TLS certificate at setup and clients pin it trust-on-first-use with fingerprint display (FR-LAN-004; CTRL-025, CTRL-026).
P0258: \newpage
P0259: 54. Functional Requirements
P0260: Each requirement follows the stimulus-response pattern ("When , the system shall "), carries a priority tier (MVP, V1, or V2) and an implementation status (Implemented, Partial, or Not started, per _context/build-status.md, verified 2026-07-06), states a deterministic acceptance oracle, and closes with a trace line naming its business goal(s) and its verifying test class in the codebase (C:\wamp64\www\Ogma-Library\tests\) or a planned test-case identifier TC-<MODULE>-NNN where no test class yet exists. The 101 identifiers below are the closed inventory fixed in _context/features.md. Requirement identifiers are defined here; all mentions elsewhere in this document are references.
P0261: 5.14.1 Library Setup and Scanning (LIB)
P0262: FR-LIB-001 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When the user selects a folder as the library root, the system shall store and display the selected root path and shall re-read that exact path on a rescan command.
P0263: Oracle: after selection, the stored root equals the selected absolute path, the path is visible in the UI, and rescan enumerates that path.
P0264: Traces: BG-001, BG-004; verified by: IngestionPipelineTests, ScanProgressTests.
P0265: FR-LIB-002 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When a scan runs, the system shall recursively discover PDF files under the library root and shall skip folders the user has marked excluded.
P0266: Oracle: for a fixture tree with PDFs at multiple depths and 1 excluded subfolder, the scan result contains every PDF outside the excluded subfolder and none inside it.
P0267: Traces: BG-001; verified by: IngestionPipelineTests.
P0268: FR-LIB-003 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When a scanned file's content hash matches an existing catalogue record whose file was renamed or moved within the root, the system shall re-match the file to that record instead of creating a duplicate.
P0269: Oracle: after rename/move and rescan, the record retains its identifier, metadata, shelves, progress, and annotations; the catalogue row count is unchanged.
P0270: Traces: BG-004; verified by: BookIdentityServiceTests, BookFileLocatorTests.
P0271: FR-LIB-004 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When a previously catalogued file is absent at scan time, the system shall mark the record removed or unavailable without deleting the user's metadata, shelves, notes, or reading progress.
P0272: Oracle: after deleting a catalogued file from disk and rescanning, the record is flagged unavailable and all user-authored data for it remains retrievable.
P0273: Traces: BG-004; verified by: UnavailableFileFlagTests.
P0274: FR-LIB-005 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When thumbnail or spine-texture generation is required, the system shall run it in background jobs that keep the UI thread within the NFR-PROD-005 budget, and shall make any generation failure visible and retryable.
P0275: Oracle: during a generation run no main-thread stall exceeds 100 ms; an injected failure produces a visible failed-job entry whose retry produces the asset.
P0276: Traces: BG-001; verified by: SpineTextureGeneratorTests, JobManagementTests.
P0277: FR-LIB-006 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When a rescan runs over unchanged files, the system shall use modification time, size, and the hash cache to skip re-hashing them.
P0278: Oracle: on a second rescan with no file changes, the count of full content-hash computations is 0 and the catalogue is unchanged.
P0279: Traces: BG-004; verified by: IncrementalRescanTests.
P0280: FR-LIB-007 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When the user requests the scan health report, the system shall list failed files, password-protected files, unsupported files, files missing thumbnails, and files with metadata gaps, each under its category.
P0281: Oracle: for a fixture containing 1 of each condition, the report lists each affected file under the correct category and counts match the fixture.
P0282: Traces: BG-001; verified by: ScanHealthTests.
P0283: 5.24.2 Catalogue Browsing (CAT)
P0284: FR-CAT-001 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When the user selects a book in any of the 4 catalogue views (3D shelf, grid, list, directory), the system shall open the same book-detail surface and the same reader.
P0285: Oracle: selecting one fixture book in each view opens an identical book-detail surface and, on open, the identical reader.
P0286: Traces: BG-001, BG-002; verified by: CatalogueGridTests, Bookshelf3DViewModelTests, ShellReaderNavigationTests.
P0287: FR-CAT-002 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When the user applies sort or filter criteria (title, author, year, status, rating, tag, shelf, availability), the system shall combine active filters conjunctively and shall clear all filters on a single clear action.
P0288: Oracle: 2 active filters return only records satisfying both; the displayed count equals the conjunction count; clear restores the unfiltered set.
P0289: Traces: BG-001; verified by: CatalogueFilterTests.
P0290: FR-CAT-003 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When the user adds a book to a virtual shelf, the system shall record membership independent of file path, and a book shall be able to belong to multiple shelves.
P0291: Oracle: a book added to 2 shelves appears in both; its file path is unchanged; removal from one shelf leaves it on the other.
P0292: Traces: BG-001, BG-002; verified by: ShelfTests.
P0293: FR-CAT-004 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When the user opens book detail, the system shall display the book's metadata across the file, bibliographic, reading, enrichment, and AI field groups.
P0294: Oracle: the detail surface shows all 5 groups and each populated field displays its stored value.
P0295: Traces: BG-001; verified by: BookDetailViewModelTests.
P0296: FR-CAT-005 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When the user confirms a bulk edit over a multi-book selection (tags, shelves, status, metadata confidence), the system shall show the pending change set before commit, apply it to every selected book, and support a single-action undo restoring the pre-edit state.
P0297: Oracle: the preview lists the change set; commit changes every selected book; one undo restores every affected book exactly (NFR-PROD-010).
P0298: Traces: BG-002; verified by: planned TC-CAT-005 (bulk edit implemented in CatalogueWriteService; no dedicated test class located).
P0299: FR-CAT-006 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When the catalogue changes, the system shall re-evaluate each smart shelf's saved filter definition and update its membership automatically.
P0300: Oracle: after adding a book matching a smart-shelf condition, the shelf lists it without user action; after the book stops matching, it is removed.
P0301: Traces: BG-001, BG-002; verified by: SmartShelfEvaluatorTests, Phase15SmartShelfPerformanceTests (implementation: SmartShelfCondition and evaluator).
P0302: FR-CAT-007 (V2 — Not started): When the user groups duplicate files or editions, the system shall associate them under one intellectual work with merge and split operations that preserve every file's own metadata and reading state.
P0303: Oracle: merging 2 Books under a Work leaves both BookFiles readable with their own progress; split restores 2 independent Books with no data loss.
P0304: Traces: BG-004; verified by: planned TC-CAT-007.
P0305: 5.34.3 Metadata Enrichment (META)
P0306: FR-META-001 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When a file is ingested or the user requests identifier detection, the system shall detect ISBN-10 and ISBN-13 candidates from the filename, XMP/DocInfo metadata, and extracted first pages, and shall normalise, check-digit-validate, and rank them.
P0307: Oracle: for a fixture whose ISBN appears in each source, every valid ISBN is detected, check-digit-invalid candidates are rejected, and the list is ordered by the documented ranking.
P0308: Traces: BG-001; verified by: IsbnDetectionTests.
P0309: FR-META-002 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When candidate ISBNs exist and enrichment runs, the system shall look them up in the Google Books and Open Library APIs and shall store source provider, retrieval timestamp, and confidence per returned value.
P0310: Oracle: a lookup against a known ISBN stores at least 1 candidate record carrying non-null source, timestamp, and confidence.
P0311: Traces: BG-001; verified by: ProviderClientTests, RateLimitedHttpClientTests.
P0312: FR-META-003 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When candidate metadata conflicts, the system shall merge through the confidence policy of Section 8.3 and shall let the user accept, reject, or manually edit each merged field.
P0313: Oracle: a merge over conflicting candidates presents the resolved value and source; a user override persists as the field value with a user-decision provenance entry.
P0314: Traces: BG-001, BG-004; verified by: ConfidenceMergeTests.
P0315: FR-META-004 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When a metadata field (title, author, cover, ISBN, publisher, description, categories) is set, the system shall record field-level provenance: source provider, confidence, retrieval date, and user decision.
P0316: Oracle: for each listed field, stored provenance carries all 4 attributes.
P0317: Traces: BG-004; verified by: ConfidenceMergeTests, MetadataApplyTests.
P0318: FR-META-005 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When the user confirms a metadata write-back, the system shall back up the original file first, show a field-level diff before commit, write the metadata, and leave the original intact on failure.
P0319: Oracle: a confirmed write-back produces a backup and updates the file; an injected write failure leaves the original byte-identical to its pre-operation state (NFR-PROD-010).
P0320: Traces: BG-004; verified by: PdfWriteBackTests (implementation: PdfWriteBackService).
P0321: FR-META-006 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When batch enrichment runs, the system shall respect configured provider rate limits, retry transient failures up to the configured count, and keep paused and failed jobs visible.
P0322: Oracle: no call exceeds the configured limit window; a transient failure is retried the configured number of times; paused and failed jobs appear in the job region.
P0323: Traces: BG-001; verified by: RateLimitedHttpClientTests, JobManagementTests.
P0324: FR-META-007 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When a book's metadata changes, the system shall recompute its metadata quality score, and the catalogue shall filter to books missing cover, ISBN, author, year, description, or categories.
P0325: Oracle: each book exposes a numeric score (schema: migration AddQualityScoreToBooks); filtering by a missing field returns exactly the books lacking it.
P0326: Traces: BG-001; verified by: HealthDashboardTests.
P0327: FR-META-008 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When the user opens the library health dashboard, the system shall report duplicates, missing metadata, unavailable files, broken covers, and index gaps, each with a per-finding remediation action.
P0328: Oracle: for a fixture containing 1 of each finding class, the dashboard lists each finding with an invocable remediation whose execution clears the finding.
P0329: Traces: BG-001, BG-004; verified by: HealthDashboardTests, HealthDashboardRenderTests (implementation: LibraryHealthService).
P0330: 5.44.4 PDF Reader (READ)
P0331: FR-READ-001 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When the user opens a previously read book from the catalogue, the system shall resume at the last reading position (page and scroll offset).
P0332: Oracle: closing at a known page and offset and reopening restores that exact page and offset.
P0333: Traces: BG-002; verified by: ReadingProgressServiceTests, ReaderSessionServiceTests.
P0334: FR-READ-002 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When the user invokes a navigation command (first, previous, next, last, jump-to-page, history back, history forward) by keyboard or UI, the system shall move to the corresponding page.
P0335: Oracle: each command, invoked both ways, lands on the specified page; history back and forward replay the visit order.
P0336: Traces: BG-002; verified by: NavigationHistoryTests, ShellReaderNavigationTests.
P0337: FR-READ-003 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When the user selects a zoom mode (fit width, fit page, fixed percentage) by control or shortcut, the system shall render at that scale and shall persist the zoom state per book or per user preference.
P0338: Oracle: each mode renders at the corresponding scale; reopening restores the persisted zoom per the configured scope.
P0339: Traces: BG-002; verified by: ReaderSessionServiceTests.
P0340: FR-READ-004 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When the user selects a display mode (single page, two-page spread, continuous scroll), the system shall render that layout and persist the mode per book.
P0341: Oracle: each mode renders the expected layout; reopening restores the last-used mode.
P0342: Traces: BG-002; verified by: ReaderViewRenderTests, ReaderSessionServiceTests.
P0343: FR-READ-005 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When the user enters full-screen reading mode, the system shall hide non-essential UI; when the user presses Escape, the system shall exit full-screen.
P0344: Oracle: full-screen hides the navigation and shelf regions; Escape restores the standard layout.
P0345: Traces: BG-002; verified by: ReaderViewRenderTests.
P0346: FR-READ-006 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When the user searches text within a document, the system shall highlight matches and list matching pages, using the PDF text layer where available.
P0347: Oracle: a search for a term in the text layer highlights each occurrence and lists every page containing it.
P0348: Traces: BG-002; verified by: InDocumentSearchTests (implementation: InDocumentSearchService, TextLayerService).
P0349: FR-READ-007 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When the user creates a labelled bookmark, the system shall persist it across close and reopen and jump to its page on activation.
P0350: Oracle: a labelled bookmark survives close/reopen, retains its label, and activation lands on its page.
P0351: Traces: BG-002; verified by: Phase09AnnotationTests (implementation: BookmarkService).
P0352: FR-READ-008 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When the user creates a highlight or note, the system shall persist it in the catalogue of record and reload the annotation overlay at its accurate position after reopen.
P0353: Oracle: a highlight over a known text region reopens at the same coordinates; the note text is unchanged.
P0354: Traces: BG-002, BG-004; verified by: Phase09AnnotationTests (implementation: AnnotationService).
P0355: FR-READ-009 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When the user opens a password-protected PDF, the system shall detect the protection and open the document through the OS credential flow only after the user consents.
P0356: Oracle: a protected fixture prompts for consent, retrieves the credential from the OS store, and renders; declining consent leaves it unopened.
P0357: Traces: BG-002; verified by: PdfiumAdapterPasswordTests, PasswordProviderTests.
P0358: FR-READ-010 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When the user enables OCR for a scanned PDF, the system shall index it as an optional background job and mark the resulting text OCR-derived and searchable.
P0359: Oracle: an OCR job over a scanned fixture produces searchable text flagged OCR-derived; a search for a known phrase returns the page.
P0360: Traces: BG-002; verified by: OcrJobProcessorTests, OcrGoldenCorpusTests.
P0361: FR-READ-011 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When the user invokes citation capture on a text selection, the system shall place a citation card carrying title, author, page, and the selected text on the clipboard.
P0362: Oracle: the clipboard card carries all 4 fields with the correct page and selection.
P0363: Traces: BG-002; verified by: Phase09AnnotationTests (implementation: CitationService).
P0364: FR-READ-012 (V2 — Not started): When the user opens split view, the system shall render two PDFs, or two locations in one PDF, side by side with independent navigation per pane.
P0365: Oracle: split view renders 2 independent panes; navigation in one does not move the other.
P0366: Traces: BG-002; verified by: planned TC-READ-012.
P0367: FR-READ-013 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When the user exports selected books, annotations, or citation cards, the system shall produce BibTeX, RIS, CSL JSON, and Markdown digest outputs.
P0368: Oracle: an export over a fixture selection yields all 4 formats, each parseable by its format's reference parser and carrying every selected item.
P0369: Traces: BG-002, BG-004; verified by: planned TC-READ-013 (export implemented in OgmaLibrary.Reader; no dedicated test class located).
P0370: FR-READ-014 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When the user records reading memory for a book, the system shall persist why the book was opened, its highlights, questions, and a finished/abandoned/saved outcome, per book.
P0371: Oracle: each recorded element survives close/reopen and is retrievable from the book's reading-memory record.
P0372: Traces: BG-002; verified by: Phase09AnnotationTests (implementation: ReadingMemoryService).
P0373: FR-READ-015 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When the user assigns annotations to a named layer (for example thesis, quotes, to-verify, teaching), the system shall show or hide annotations per layer and export per layer.
P0374: Oracle: hiding a layer removes exactly its annotations from the overlay; a per-layer export contains only that layer's annotations.
P0375: Traces: BG-002; verified by: Phase09AnnotationTests (implementation: AnnotationLayerService).
P0376: 5.54.5 Search and Indexing (SEARCH)
P0377: FR-SEARCH-001 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When the user types a metadata query, the system shall return results while typing within the NFR-OGMA-003 budget (150 ms at P95) on a 2,000-book corpus.
P0378: Oracle: a query-replay benchmark over the gc-perf-2000 corpus reports P95 at or below 150 ms on W-REF-01 (formal acceptance at Phase 20; pre-Phase 20 measurements are trend-only).
P0379: Traces: BG-002; verified by: MetadataSearchServiceTests.
P0380: FR-SEARCH-002 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When indexing runs, the system shall maintain an FTS5 index covering extracted text, notes, tags, descriptions, and tables of contents.
P0381: Oracle: a term present only in each listed source is found by full-text search after indexing; the index manager reports source coverage.
P0382: Traces: BG-002; verified by: FtsIndexServiceTests (implementation: FtsIndexService).
P0383: FR-SEARCH-003 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When search returns a result, the system shall state its match location as one of title, author, note, text-page, or semantic hit.
P0384: Oracle: for a query matching in each location type, every result row displays the correct label.
P0385: Traces: BG-002; verified by: MatchLocationServiceTests.
P0386: FR-SEARCH-004 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When the user submits a natural-language query, the system shall return semantically matching books through embedding search.
P0387: Oracle: a fixed natural-language query whose intent matches a fixture book lacking the literal terms returns that book among the ranked semantic results, reproducibly for a fixed corpus and embedding model.
P0388: Traces: BG-002, BG-003; verified by: SemanticSearchServiceTests, CosineSimilarityServiceTests (implementation: SemanticSearchService).
P0389: FR-SEARCH-005 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When hybrid search runs, the system shall produce a ranking that blends exact match, recency, reading status, rating, and semantic similarity per the documented weighting.
P0390: Oracle: a fixed query against a fixed corpus produces a deterministic ranked order reflecting each signal; the order is reproducible across runs.
P0391: Traces: BG-002, BG-003; verified by: HybridRankingServiceTests (implementation: HybridRankingService).
P0392: FR-SEARCH-006 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When the user opens the index manager, the system shall display indexing progress, indexed-book count, pending OCR, failed extractions, and index size.
P0393: Oracle: each figure is displayed and reconciles with the underlying index state for a known fixture.
P0394: Traces: BG-002; verified by: IndexManagerServiceTests (implementation: IndexManagerService).
P0395: 5.64.6 AI Advisor (AI)
P0396: FR-AI-001 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When the user disables AI, the system shall require no provider key and shall block no core reading function; the default provider is the disabled provider.
P0397: Oracle: with AI disabled and no key configured, every LIB, CAT, META, READ, and SEARCH MVP function operates; AiDisabledProvider is the resolved provider.
P0398: Traces: BG-005; verified by: AiGatewayTests.
P0399: FR-AI-002 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When the user selects an AI provider (OpenAI-compatible, Anthropic-compatible, DeepSeek-compatible, or Ollama local), the system shall route all calls through the single IAiProvider contract.
P0400: Oracle: each provider selection routes through the gateway; switching providers requires no change outside provider configuration.
P0401: Traces: BG-003; verified by: AiProviderAdapterTests, AiContractTests.
P0402: FR-AI-003 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When the user states an intent, the system shall recommend books drawn only from the catalogued collection, as ranked cards each carrying an explanation and a confidence value.
P0403: Oracle (structural, deterministic): every returned card references a catalogued book, carries a non-empty explanation naming the matching field or content, and a confidence value within [0, 1]. Recommendation relevance quality is assessed separately by the Phase 13 AI evaluation harness, not by this oracle.
P0404: Traces: BG-003; verified by: AdvisorServiceTests, RecommendationPipelineTests, Phase13EvaluationHarnessTests.
P0405: FR-AI-004 (MVP — Code/test evidence): When cloud AI is enabled in the default metadata-only mode, the system shall send only title, author, tags, categories, descriptions, and notes off-device.
P0406: Oracle: a captured outbound payload in metadata-only mode contains only the listed fields and no document content (NFR-PROD-011).
P0407: Traces: BG-005; verified by: AiGatewayIntegrationTests, PayloadPreviewViewModelTests.
P0408: FR-AI-005 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When content-aware mode is requested, the system shall require explicit opt-in per library or per query and shall show a privacy label and previewed payload before any content chunk is sent.
P0409: Oracle: a content-aware request is blocked until opt-in is recorded; absent opt-in, no content chunk leaves the device.
P0410: Traces: BG-005; verified by: PayloadPreviewViewModelTests, AiGatewayIntegrationTests.
P0411: FR-AI-006 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When the local embedding path (Ollama) is active, the system shall generate embeddings and serve semantic search without any off-device request.
P0412: Oracle: with the network interface disabled, embedding generation and semantic search complete and the egress monitor records 0 off-device requests.
P0413: Traces: BG-003, BG-005; verified by: EmbeddingGenerationServiceTests, EmbeddingGenerationWorkerTests.
P0414: FR-AI-007 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When the user requests a reading plan, the system shall return an ordered sequence of catalogued books with a rationale per step, an estimated difficulty, and checkpoints.
P0415: Oracle (structural, deterministic): the response contains all 4 named elements and every sequenced book is catalogued. Pedagogical quality is assessed by the Phase 13 AI evaluation harness, not by this oracle.
P0416: Traces: BG-003; verified by: ReadingPlanPipelineTests.
P0417: FR-AI-008 (V2 — Not started): When AI answer mode is used, the system shall cite local evidence — book title, page or chunk reference, and confidence — for each answer.
P0418: Oracle: an answer-mode response carries at least 1 citation naming a catalogued book, a page or chunk reference, and a confidence value.
P0419: Traces: BG-003; verified by: planned TC-AI-008 (deliberate NotImplementedException stub in AdvisorService; the classroom variant is FR-ADMIN-011).
P0420: FR-AI-009 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When the user deletes AI query history or disables retention, the system shall remove the history for the chosen scope and stop further recording.
P0421: Oracle: a delete removes the scope's history rows; with retention disabled, subsequent queries record nothing (NFR-PROD-014).
P0422: Traces: BG-005; verified by: AiPersistenceTests, EmbeddingErasureTests.
P0423: FR-AI-010 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When a cloud call completes, the system shall record and display the model identifier, token or unit usage, and an estimated cost that reconciles with the provider response.
P0424: Oracle: each cloud call's displayed usage equals the provider-reported usage; the cost estimate applies the configured price table to that usage.
P0425: Traces: BG-003, BG-005; verified by: AiGatewayTests.
P0426: FR-AI-011 (V1 — Code/test evidence): When the user opens the Privacy Center, the system shall list provider keys, recent AI calls with exact payloads and per-call privacy tier, delete controls for history and embeddings, and an audit-log export.
P0427: Oracle: after 1 metadata-only call, the Privacy Center shows that call with its exact payload and tier; the delete control removes it; the export contains the matching audit entries.
P0428: Traces: BG-005; verified by: PrivacyCenterViewModelTests.
P0429: 5.74.7 LAN Library Host Mode (LAN)
P0430: The LAN module is V2 classroom scope, implemented in grand-plan Phase 16 (KestrelHostModeListener; schema: migration Phase16LanHostTables adding HostModeSettings and HostClientSessions). Requirement wording follows _context/features.md; per-requirement verification detail is in docs/plans/grand-plan/phase-16/README.md.
P0431: FR-LAN-001 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When an administrator enables host mode, the system shall start the inbound listener explicitly, reversibly, and with an audit entry; the default install shall run no inbound listener.
P0432: Oracle: toggle on binds the listener; toggle off releases the port; each toggle writes an audit record; a default install binds no port.
P0433: Traces: BG-002, BG-005; verified by: LanHostScaffoldTests, LanBindAddressSelectorTests.
P0434: FR-LAN-002 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When host mode is active, the system shall serve catalogue metadata and PDF content streams over TLS on the local network only.
P0435: Oracle: catalogue and stream endpoints respond only over TLS on LAN-scoped bind addresses; a plaintext request is refused.
P0436: Traces: BG-002, BG-005; verified by: LanHostEndpointTests, LanClientAddressPolicyTests.
P0437: FR-LAN-003 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When host mode is active, the system shall advertise the host via mDNS (_ogma-library._tcp.local) so clients can discover it on the LAN.
P0438: Oracle: the advertised service resolves on a test client; discovery lists the host by name.
P0439: Traces: BG-002; verified by: MdnsAdvertiserTests, MdnsResolverTests, DiscoveryServiceTests.
P0440: FR-LAN-004 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When host mode first starts, the system shall provision a TLS certificate, and clients shall record it in a host trust store using trust-on-first-use with fingerprint display.
P0441: Oracle: first start creates a valid certificate whose fingerprint matches the stored value; a client accepting it pins it; a subsequent different certificate is rejected with a warning.
P0442: Traces: BG-005; verified by: LanHostCertificateProvisionerTests, HostTrustServiceTests.
P0443: FR-LAN-005 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When a client enrolls, the system shall authenticate it with per-profile credentials issued through join codes (QR).
P0444: Oracle: a valid join code enrolls a profile whose token authenticates subsequent requests; an invalid or consumed code is refused.
P0445: Traces: BG-005; verified by: ClassroomJoinParserTests, ClassroomCredentialStoreTests.
P0446: FR-LAN-006 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When a client requests PDF content, the system shall serve range-request streams and shall validate every resolved path server-side against the published library root.
P0447: Oracle: a range request returns the requested byte range of a published file; a request resolving outside the published root is refused and logged.
P0448: Traces: BG-002, BG-005; verified by: LanBookFileResolverTests, LanPageRenderLimiterTests.
P0449: FR-LAN-007 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When the administrator opens session management, the system shall list active client sessions, enforce the configured session limit, and revoke a selected session.
P0450: Oracle: active sessions are listed from HostClientSessions; a session beyond the limit is refused; a revoked session's next request is rejected.
P0451: Traces: BG-005; verified by: LanHostPersistenceTests, HostSharingViewModelTests.
P0452: FR-LAN-008 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When the host becomes unavailable, the system shall degrade non-destructively: clients keep local reading state and re-sync on reconnect.
P0453: Oracle: dropping the host mid-session leaves client-side progress, bookmarks, and notes intact; reconnect syncs them without loss.
P0454: Traces: BG-002, BG-004; verified by: ClassroomConnectionServiceTests, OfflineCacheServiceTests.
P0455: FR-LAN-009 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When a client enumerates the shared library, the system shall expose only admin-published folders and shelves; unpublished content shall never be enumerable.
P0456: Oracle: a book in an unpublished folder appears in no client catalogue response and no asset or stream endpoint serves it.
P0457: Traces: BG-005; verified by: SchoolAdminCatalogueServiceTests, LanBookFileResolverTests.
P0458: FR-LAN-010 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When any LAN request (connection, stream, denial) is processed, the system shall append a host activity audit record exportable by the administrator.
P0459: Oracle: n requests produce n AuditEvents rows carrying client identity and resource; the export contains them.
P0460: Traces: BG-005; verified by: AuditRepositoryTests, LanHostEndpointTests.
P0461: 5.84.8 Classroom Client Mode (CLIENT)
P0462: The CLIENT module is V2 classroom scope, implemented in grand-plan Phase 17 (ClassroomClient). Requirement wording follows _context/features.md; verification detail is in docs/plans/grand-plan/phase-17/README.md.
P0463: FR-CLIENT-001 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When the user selects a discovered host or enters a host address, the system shall connect and browse the shared catalogue.
P0464: Oracle: a discovered or manually entered host connects, authenticates, and populates the catalogue grid from the host projection; the mode persists across restart.
P0465: Traces: BG-001, BG-002; verified by: ClassroomConnectionServiceTests, ClassroomCatalogueReadModelTests.
P0466: FR-CLIENT-002 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When a student profile is active, the system shall store reading state (progress, bookmarks, notes) privately per profile.
P0467: Oracle: state written under profile A is absent from profile B's store on the same machine; the private store path is keyed by profile.
P0468: Traces: BG-002, BG-005; verified by: ProfileServiceTests, StudentPrivateRepositoryTests.
P0469: FR-CLIENT-003 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When a profile is created or selected, the system shall assign one of the roles student, teacher, or administrator and shall scope capabilities to that role.
P0470: Oracle: a student profile cannot invoke admin actions; a teacher profile reaches the class dashboard; the role travels in the session token.
P0471: Traces: BG-005; verified by: ProfileServiceTests.
P0472: FR-CLIENT-004 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When a client reads a shared book, the system shall stream content and shall never require a full-file copy for reading.
P0473: Oracle: opening a shared book renders pages from streamed ranges; no complete source file is materialised on the client for reading.
P0474: Traces: BG-002, BG-004; verified by: ClassroomBookFileLocatorTests, ClassroomBookFileMaterializerTests.
P0475: FR-CLIENT-005 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When recently read books are cached offline, the system shall enforce an explicit cache size limit and provide a purge control.
P0476: Oracle: cache growth stops at the configured limit with eviction of the oldest entries; purge empties the cache.
P0477: Traces: BG-002; verified by: OfflineCacheServiceTests, CachingLibraryHostClientTests.
P0478: FR-CLIENT-006 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When reading state syncs to the host, the system shall resolve conflicts latest-writer-wins per field with no silent data loss.
P0479: Oracle: 2 conflicting field writes resolve to the later timestamp; the losing value is surfaced, not silently discarded.
P0480: Traces: BG-002, BG-004; verified by: ClassroomSyncServiceTests, ClassroomSyncBlobCodecTests.
P0481: FR-CLIENT-007 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When a client searches, the system shall execute the query against the host index and shall return results carrying the same match-location explanations as local search (FR-SEARCH-003).
P0482: Oracle: a client query returns host-index results and each row displays a match-location label from the same label set.
P0483: Traces: BG-002, BG-003; verified by: StudentSmartSearchViewModelTests, ClassroomCatalogueReadModelTests.
P0484: FR-CLIENT-008 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When multiple students use the same host, the system shall keep each student's reading state and AI history invisible to other students.
P0485: Oracle: student A's state and AI history are unreachable from student B's session by any client or host endpoint.
P0486: Traces: BG-005; verified by: StudentPrivateRepositoryTests.
P0487: FR-CLIENT-009 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When a profile is enrolled or revoked, the system shall apply the change at the profile's next connection; a revoked client shall lose host access but keep its local notes.
P0488: Oracle: a revoked profile's next connection is refused; its locally stored notes remain readable in local mode.
P0489: Traces: BG-004, BG-005; verified by: SchoolProfileEnrollmentServiceTests.
P0490: FR-CLIENT-010 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When a client operates against the shared library, the system shall permit no client mutation of host files or host metadata.
P0491: Oracle: no client endpoint accepts a write to host catalogue metadata or files; attempted writes are refused and audited.
P0492: Traces: BG-004; verified by: ClassroomModeServiceTests, LibraryHostHttpClientTests.
P0493: FR-CLIENT-011 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When the host is unreachable, the system shall show the connection state and degrade to the client's own local library.
P0494: Oracle: dropping the LAN mid-session updates the connection indicator and the local library remains browsable and readable.
P0495: Traces: BG-002; verified by: ClassroomConnectionServiceTests, CachingLibraryHostClientTests.
P0496: FR-CLIENT-012 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When a teacher opens the class dashboard, the system shall show class-level aggregate reading data, and any per-student visibility shall be disclosed to the affected students.
P0497: Oracle: the dashboard renders aggregates for the enrolled class; where a per-student figure is shown, the student's client displays the disclosure notice.
P0498: Traces: BG-002, BG-005; verified by: planned TC-CLIENT-012.
P0499: FR-CLIENT-013 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When a profile opens a shelf or book, the system shall honour host entitlements defining which shelves and books that profile may open.
P0500: Oracle: a book outside the profile's entitlement is not openable and its stream request is refused; an entitled book opens.
P0501: Traces: BG-005; verified by: planned TC-CLIENT-013 (entitlement checks implemented host-side; no dedicated client test class located).

P0524: Traces: BG-005; verified by: PayloadPreviewViewModelTests, StudentSmartSearchViewModelTests.
P0525: FR-ADMIN-008 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When a per-student or per-class AI quota is exhausted, the system shall refuse further AI queries for that scope without making a provider call.
P0526: Oracle: a query beyond quota returns a quota-exhausted response and the provider call count does not increase.
P0527: Traces: BG-005; verified by: SchoolAiPolicyServiceTests (schema: SchoolAiEntitlements, AiUsageLedger).
P0528: FR-ADMIN-009 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When a student exceeds the per-student rate limit, the system shall reject the excess requests with a retry-after indication; requests below the limit shall succeed.
P0529: Oracle: requests above the per-minute limit are rejected with retry-after; requests below it succeed.
P0530: Traces: BG-005; verified by: SchoolAiPolicyServiceTests.
P0531: FR-ADMIN-010 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When the administrator opens the usage dashboard, the system shall show per-student and per-class AI query counts, cost, and quota utilization derived from the usage ledger.
P0532: Oracle: dashboard figures equal the corresponding AiUsageLedger and AuditEvents aggregates for a known fixture.
P0533: Traces: BG-003, BG-005; verified by: SchoolUsageDashboardServiceTests, SchoolAdminPanelRenderTests.
P0534: FR-ADMIN-011 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When classroom answer mode responds, the system shall cite only evidence present in the host catalogue.
P0535: Oracle: every citation in an answer names a book present in the host catalogue; a response containing any other reference fails.
P0536: Traces: BG-003; verified by: ClassroomAnswerGrounderTests.
P0537: FR-ADMIN-012 (V2 — Code/test evidence): When a student deletes their own AI query history, the system shall clear it from the student's private store.
P0538: Oracle: after deletion, the student's AI history store contains 0 rows for that student.
P0539: Traces: BG-005; verified by: SchoolAiHistoryManagementServiceTests.

P0542: Traces: BG-005; verified by: SchoolAiHistoryManagementServiceTests.
P0543: 5.104.10 Extension and Interoperability (EXT)
P0544: FR-EXT-001 (V2 — Partial): When a plugin registers against a metadata, export, OCR, or AI provider interface, the system shall load it behind the corresponding contract, and all extension off-device traffic shall route through the AI gateway privacy tiers.
